    <!-- LÓGICA DE SESIÓN (para barra lateral) -->
    <script type="module">
        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";

// Inicializar el sidebar dinámicamente al cargar la vista
initSidebar("sidebar-container");
        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";
        import { doc, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        const userEmailSpan = document.getElementById("userEmail");
        const userRoleSpan  = document.getElementById("userRole");
    
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }
            userEmailSpan.innerText = user.email;
            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));
                if (userSnap.exists()) {
                    const userData = userSnap.data();
                    const isAdmin = userData.role === "admin";
                    userRoleSpan.innerText = isAdmin ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador";
                    if (!isAdmin) {
                        document.querySelectorAll("[data-admin-only]").forEach(el => el.remove());
                    }
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error sesión:", err);
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">

<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">

  <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="./img/favicon.svg">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#4f46e5">
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css">
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <style>
    body { font-family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', sans-serif; -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; }
    .glass { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7); backdrop-filter: blur(10px); }
    .custom-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 5px; }
    .custom-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }
    .custom-scroll::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #cbd5e1; border-radius: 10px; }
  </style>
</head>

<body class="bg-slate-950 min-h-screen flex flex-col text-slate-100 antialiased">
  <div id="sidebar-container" class="h-full flex shrink-0"></div>
  <!-- OVERLAY DE CARGA -->
  <div id="bootOverlay" class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950 z-[100] flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-3">
    <div class="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 tracking-wider uppercase">Calculando agencias cercanas...</p>
  </div>

  <header class="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-700 to-violet-800 text-white pt-8 pb-12 px-6 rounded-b-[2.5rem] shadow-2xl">
    <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
      <div class="space-y-1">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span class="flex h-2 w-2">
            <span class="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75"></span>
            <span class="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500"></span>
          </span>
          <p id="estadoGPS" class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-bold opacity-80">Rastreo GPS Activo</p>
        </div>
        <h1 class="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">Agencias Cercanas</h1>
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap-2">
        <a href="index.html" class="w-10 h-10 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white transition">
          <i class="fa fa-home"></i>
        </a>
        <a href="mapa.html" class="w-10 h-10 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white transition">
          <i class="fa fa-map-location-dot"></i>
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main class="max-w-4xl w-full mx-auto flex-1 -mt-8 px-4 space-y-6 pb-12">

    <!-- AGENCIA MÁS CERCANA (DESTACADA) -->
    <div id="closestAgencyContainer">
      <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-xl animate-pulse">
        <div class="h-4 bg-slate-800 w-24 rounded mb-4"></div>
        <div class="h-8 bg-slate-800 w-48 rounded mb-6"></div>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div class="h-12 bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl"></div>
          <div class="h-12 bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- BUSCADOR -->
    <div class="relative">
      <span class="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-4 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-slate-500">
        <i class="fa fa-search"></i>
      </span>
      <input type="text" id="inputSearch" placeholder="Buscar por código, dirección o zona..."
        class="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-4 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl text-sm text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 transition-all shadow-inner placeholder:text-slate-500 font-medium">
    </div>

    <!-- LISTADO DE AGENCIAS ORDENADAS POR DISTANCIA -->
    <div>
      <h3 class="px-2 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-[0.2em] mb-3">
        Listado por Distancia
      </h3>
      <div id="agencyList" class="space-y-3">
        <!-- Dinámico -->
      </div>
    </div>

  </main>

  <script type="module">
    import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";
    import { requireAuth } from "./js/auth.guard.js";
    import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";              // 👈 AGREGAR
initSidebar("sidebar-container");                            // 👈 AGREGAR
    import { collection, getDocs } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { onAuthStateChanged, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

    const bootOverlay = document.getElementById("bootOverlay");
    const closestAgencyContainer = document.getElementById("closestAgencyContainer");
    const agencyList = document.getElementById("agencyList");
    const inputSearch = document.getElementById("inputSearch");
    const estadoGPS = document.getElementById("estadoGPS");

    let allAgencies = [];
    let userCoords = null;

    // Verificar sesión con el guard
    requireAuth().then(session => {
        if (!session) return;
        initGeolocatedProximity();
    });

    function initGeolocatedProximity() {
        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            estadoGPS.textContent = "GPS no soportado";
            loadAgenciesWithoutGPS();
            return;
        }

        navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
            (position) => {
                userCoords = {
                    lat: position.coords.latitude,
                    lng: position.coords.longitude
                };
                estadoGPS.textContent = `GPS Activo (±${Math.round(position.coords.accuracy)}m)`;
                if (allAgencies.length > 0) renderProximityData();
            },
            (error) => {
                console.warn("Error de geolocalización:", error.message);
                estadoGPS.textContent = "GPS Inactivo (Usando ubicación por defecto)";
                // Coordenadas por defecto (Ej: Centro del país o San Juan)
                userCoords = { lat: 18.7357, lng: -70.1627 };
                if (allAgencies.length > 0) renderProximityData();
            },
            { enableHighAccuracy: true, maximumAge: 10000, timeout: 15000 }
        );

        loadAgencies();
    }

    async function loadAgencies() {
        try {
            const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, "agencias"));
            allAgencies = [];

            snap.forEach(docSnap => {
                const data = docSnap.data();
                if (data.enabled === false) return;
                const lat = Number(data.lat ?? data.latitud ?? 0);
                const lng = Number(data.lng ?? data.longitud ?? 0);
                if (!lat || !lng) return;

                allAgencies.push({
                    id: docSnap.id,
                    idReal: data.idReal || data.idreal || "S/N",
                    zona: data.zona || "GENERAL",
                    direccion: data.direccion || "Sin dirección",
                    lat,
                    lng
                });
            });

            bootOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
            renderProximityData();

        } catch (err) {
            console.error("Error al cargar agencias:", err);
            bootOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
            agencyList.innerHTML = `<p class="text-center text-rose-400 py-8 text-xs">Error al cargar agencias desde Firestore.</p>`;
        }
    }

    function renderProximityData() {
        if (!userCoords || allAgencies.length === 0) return;

        const queryText = inputSearch.value.toLowerCase().trim();

        // Calcular distancias con Haversine
        const evaluated = allAgencies.map(agency => {
            const distKm = calculateDistance(userCoords.lat, userCoords.lng, agency.lat, agency.lng);
            return { ...agency, distKm };
        }).sort((a, b) => a.distKm - b.distKm);

        const filtered = evaluated.filter(a => {
            if (!queryText) return true;
            return a.idReal.toLowerCase().includes(queryText) ||
                   a.direccion.toLowerCase().includes(queryText) ||
                   a.zona.toLowerCase().includes(queryText);
        });

        if (filtered.length === 0) {
            closestAgencyContainer.innerHTML = "";
            agencyList.innerHTML = `<p class="text-center text-slate-500 py-8 text-xs">No se encontraron agencias.</p>`;
            return;
        }

        // La más cercana es la primera del array ordenado global
        const closest = evaluated[0];
        renderClosestCard(closest);

        // Listado general filtrado
        agencyList.innerHTML = "";
        filtered.forEach(agency => {
            agencyList.appendChild(buildAgencyCard(agency));
        });
    }

    function renderClosestCard(agency) {
        const mapsUrl = `https://www.google.com/maps?q=${agency.lat},${agency.lng}`;
        closestAgencyContainer.innerHTML = `
            <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-900/90 to-slate-900 border border-indigo-500/30 rounded-[2rem] p-6 shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
                <div class="absolute -right-10 -bottom-10 w-40 h-40 bg-indigo-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none"></div>
                <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                    <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300 text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider border border-indigo-500/30">
                        Más cercana a ti
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-emerald-400 font-mono font-black text-sm">
                        ${formatDistance(agency.distKm)}
                    </span>
                </div>
                <h2 class="text-2xl font-black text-white mb-1">${escapeHtml(agency.idReal)}</h2>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fa fa-location-dot text-indigo-400"></i> ${escapeHtml(agency.direccion)}
                </p>
                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <a href="${mapsUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"
                       class="py-3 px-4 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl text-center transition shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/30 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                        <i class="fa fa-diamond-turn-right"></i> Navegar
                    </a>
                    <a href="#" disabled
                       class="py-3 px-4 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl text-center transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                        <i class="fa fa-route"></i> proximamente
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    }

    function buildAgencyCard(agency) {
        const mapsUrl = `https://www.google.com/maps?q=${agency.lat},${agency.lng}`;
        const card = document.createElement("div");
        card.className = "bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 transition";

        card.innerHTML = `
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3.5 min-w-0">
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center text-indigo-400 font-black text-xs shrink-0">
                    <i class="fa fa-store"></i>
                </div>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-white truncate">${escapeHtml(agency.idReal)}</h4>
                        <span class="text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">${escapeHtml(agency.zona)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">${escapeHtml(agency.direccion)}</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
                <div class="text-right">
                    <span class="block text-xs font-black text-emerald-400 font-mono">${formatDistance(agency.distKm)}</span>
                </div>
                <a href="${mapsUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"
                   class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-indigo-600 text-slate-300 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition text-xs"
                   title="Abrir en Mapa">
                    <i class="fa fa-location-arrow"></i>
                </a>
            </div>
        `;
        return card;
    }

    function calculateDistance(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
        const R = 6371; // Radio de la tierra en km
        const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;
        const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;
        const a =
            Math.sin(dLat/2) * Math.sin(dLat/2) +
            Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
            Math.sin(dLon/2) * Math.sin(dLon/2);
        const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1-a));
        return R * c;
    }

    function formatDistance(km) {
        if (km < 1) return `${Math.round(km * 1000)} m`;
        return `${km.toFixed(1)} km`;
    }

    function escapeHtml(str) {
        return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
            "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;"
        }[c]));
    }

    inputSearch.addEventListener("input", () => renderProximityData());
  </script>
</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: configuracion.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es" class="h-full bg-slate-950">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport"
        content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, viewport-fit=cover, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#020617">

                            <option value="skip">Omitir duplicados (mantiene existente)</option>

                        <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Un "duplicado" se detecta por el campo
                            <code>idReal</code>.</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Acciones -->
                <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-end items-center gap-3">
                    <button id="btnProcessData"

                        <i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass text-slate-400"></i> Procesar y Previsualizar
                    </button>
                    <button id="btnSaveAgencies" disabled
                        class="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-600 disabled:border-slate-800 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-brand-500/20 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up"></i> Guardar en Firestore

                <!-- Progress -->
                <div id="progressContainer" class="hidden mt-6">

                        <span id="progressLabel">Procesando registros...</span>
                        <span id="progressText">0%</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-full h-3 overflow-hidden">
                        <div id="progressBar" class="bg-brand-500 h-3 rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                            style="width: 0%"></div>

            <!-- Preview Card -->
            <div id="previewCard"

                <div

                        </tbody>

    <!-- Modal Agencia Individual -->
    <div id="singleAgencyModal"

        <div
            class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-lg overflow-hidden shadow-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto custom-scroll">

                    <input type="text" id="singleIdReal" required placeholder="Ej: 8125840"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500 font-mono">
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label for="singleSelectZona" class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Zona *</label>
                    <select id="singleSelectZona" required
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500 font-semibold">
                        <option value="">-- Seleccionar Zona --</option>
                    </select>

                <div>
                    <label for="singleDireccion" class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Dirección</label>
                    <input type="text" id="singleDireccion" placeholder="Ej: Av. Independencia 123"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                </div>

                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <label for="singleLat" class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Latitud</label>
                        <input type="number" step="any" id="singleLat" placeholder="18.4861"
                            class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500 font-mono">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label for="singleLng" class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Longitud</label>
                        <input type="number" step="any" id="singleLng" placeholder="-69.9312"
                            class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500 font-mono">
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="pt-4 flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-slate-800">
                    <button type="button" id="btnCancelModal"
                        class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold rounded-xl">
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button type="submit" id="btnSaveSingle"
                        class="px-5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:bg-slate-700 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow transition-all flex items-center gap-2">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk" id="btnSaveSingleIcon"></i>
                        <span id="btnSaveSingleText">Guardar Agencia</span>
                    </button>

    <!-- Librerías -->
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

    <!-- Lógica Principal -->
    <script type="module">
        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";
        // Inicializar el sidebar dinámicamente al cargar la vista
        initSidebar("sidebar-container");

        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

        import {
            collection,
            doc,
            getDoc,
            getDocs,
            setDoc,
            updateDoc,
            query,
            where,
            writeBatch,
            serverTimestamp
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

        import { onAuthStateChanged, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        // DOM
        const bootOverlay = document.getElementById("bootOverlay");
        const mainContent = document.getElementById("mainContent");
        const dropZone = document.getElementById("dropZone");
        const excelFileInput = document.getElementById("excelFileInput");
        const fileNameDisplay = document.getElementById("fileNameDisplay");
        const fileNameText = document.getElementById("fileNameText");
        const btnClearFile = document.getElementById("btnClearFile");
        const rawTextarea = document.getElementById("rawTextarea");
        const selectZona = document.getElementById("selectZona");
        const importStrategy = document.getElementById("importStrategy");
        const btnProcessData = document.getElementById("btnProcessData");
        const btnSaveAgencies = document.getElementById("btnSaveAgencies");
        const previewCard = document.getElementById("previewCard");
        const previewTableBody = document.getElementById("previewTableBody");
        const recordCountBadge = document.getElementById("recordCountBadge");
        const progressContainer = document.getElementById("progressContainer");
        const progressBar = document.getElementById("progressBar");
        const progressText = document.getElementById("progressText");
        const progressLabel = document.getElementById("progressLabel");
        const tabExcelBtn = document.getElementById("tabExcelBtn");
        const tabTextareaBtn = document.getElementById("tabTextareaBtn");
        const tabExcel = document.getElementById("tabExcel");
        const tabTextarea = document.getElementById("tabTextarea");

        const singleAgencyModal = document.getElementById("singleAgencyModal");
        const singleAgencyForm = document.getElementById("singleAgencyForm");
        const singleSelectZona = document.getElementById("singleSelectZona");
        const btnSaveSingle = document.getElementById("btnSaveSingle");
        const btnSaveSingleIcon = document.getElementById("btnSaveSingleIcon");
        const btnSaveSingleText = document.getElementById("btnSaveSingleText");

        const userEmailSpan = document.getElementById("userEmail");
        const userRoleSpan = document.getElementById("userRole");

        let parsedAgencies = [];
        let selectedFile = null;
        let activeTab = "excel";
        let existingZones = [];
        let isAdmin = false;

        const TAB_ACTIVE = "tab-btn flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-5 text-xs font-bold border-b-2 border-brand-500 text-brand-400 transition-all";
        const TAB_INACTIVE = "tab-btn flex items-center gap-2 py-3 px-5 text-xs font-bold border-b-2 border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 transition-all";

        function setActiveTab(type) {
            activeTab = type;
            if (type === "excel") {
                tabExcel.classList.remove("hidden");
                tabTextarea.classList.add("hidden");
                tabExcelBtn.className = TAB_ACTIVE;
                tabTextareaBtn.className = TAB_INACTIVE;
                tabExcelBtn.setAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
                tabTextareaBtn.setAttribute("aria-selected", "false");
            } else {
                tabTextarea.classList.remove("hidden");
                tabExcel.classList.add("hidden");
                tabTextareaBtn.className = TAB_ACTIVE;
                tabExcelBtn.className = TAB_INACTIVE;
                tabTextareaBtn.setAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
                tabExcelBtn.setAttribute("aria-selected", "false");
            }
        }
        tabExcelBtn.addEventListener("click", () => setActiveTab("excel"));
        tabTextareaBtn.addEventListener("click", () => setActiveTab("textarea"));

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }

            userEmailSpan.innerText = user.email;

            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));
                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Tu cuenta no está registrada. Contacta al administrador.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const userData = userSnap.data();
                if (userData.enabled === false) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Tu usuario ha sido inhabilitado.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                isAdmin = userData.role === "admin";
                userRoleSpan.innerText = isAdmin ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador";
                userRoleSpan.className = isAdmin
                    ? "text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wider"
                    : "text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider";

                if (!isAdmin) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Acceso denegado: solo administradores pueden importar datos.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    window.location.replace("index.html");
                    return;
                }

                bootOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
                mainContent.classList.remove("hidden");

                await loadZones();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al validar sesión:", err);
                window.location.replace("index.html");
            }
        });

        async function loadZones() {
            try {
                const zonasSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, "zonas"));
                const zonesSet = new Set();
                zonasSnap.forEach(d => {
                    const id = String(d.id || "").toUpperCase().trim();
                    const nombre = d.data().nombre ? String(d.data().nombre).toUpperCase().trim() : "";
                    if (id) zonesSet.add(id);
                    if (nombre) zonesSet.add(nombre);
                });
                existingZones = Array.from(zonesSet).sort();
                if (existingZones.length === 0) existingZones = ["GENERAL"];
                populateZoneDropdowns(existingZones);
            } catch (err) {
                existingZones = ["GENERAL"];
                populateZoneDropdowns(existingZones);
            }
        }

        function populateZoneDropdowns(zones) {
            selectZona.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Sin zona global (usa columna) --</option>`;
            zones.forEach(z => {
                selectZona.innerHTML += `<option value="${escapeHtml(z)}">${escapeHtml(z)}</option>`;
            });

            singleSelectZona.innerHTML = `<option value="">-- Seleccionar Zona --</option>`;
            zones.forEach(z => {
                singleSelectZona.innerHTML += `<option value="${escapeHtml(z)}">${escapeHtml(z)}</option>`;
            });
        }

        document.getElementById("btnOpenSingleModal").addEventListener("click", () => {
            singleAgencyForm.reset();
            singleAgencyModal.classList.remove("hidden");
            setTimeout(() => document.getElementById("singleIdReal").focus(), 50);
        });

        const closeModal = () => {
            singleAgencyModal.classList.add("hidden");
            singleAgencyForm.reset();
        };
        document.getElementById("btnCloseModal").addEventListener("click", closeModal);
        document.getElementById("btnCancelModal").addEventListener("click", closeModal);
        singleAgencyModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            if (e.target === singleAgencyModal) closeModal();
        });

        singleAgencyForm.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (btnSaveSingle.disabled) return;

            const idReal = document.getElementById("singleIdReal").value.trim();
            const zona = singleSelectZona.value;
            const direccion = document.getElementById("singleDireccion").value.trim();
            const lat = parseFloat(document.getElementById("singleLat").value) || 0;
            const lng = parseFloat(document.getElementById("singleLng").value) || 0;

            if (!idReal || !zona) {
                Swal.fire("Atención", "Por favor completa el ID Real y la Zona.", "warning");
                return;
            }

            btnSaveSingle.disabled = true;
            btnSaveSingleIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin";
            btnSaveSingleText.textContent = "Guardando...";

            try {
                const q = query(collection(db, "agencias"), where("idReal", "==", idReal));
                const snap = await getDocs(q);

                if (!snap.empty) {
                    const updateConfirm = await Swal.fire({
                        title: "Agencia existente",
                        text: `Ya existe una agencia con idReal ${idReal}. ¿Deseas actualizarla?`,
                        icon: "warning",
                        showCancelButton: true,
                        confirmButtonText: "Sí, actualizar",
                        cancelButtonText: "Cancelar"
                    });

                    if (!updateConfirm.isConfirmed) {
                        btnSaveSingle.disabled = false;
                        btnSaveSingleIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-floppy-disk";
                        btnSaveSingleText.textContent = "Guardar Agencia";
                        return;
                    }

                    const existingDocId = snap.docs[0].id;
                    await updateDoc(doc(db, "agencias", existingDocId), {
                        zona, direccion, lat, lng, updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                    });
                    Swal.fire("Actualizado", "La agencia ha sido actualizada correctamente.", "success");
                } else {
                    await setDoc(doc(collection(db, "agencias")), {
                        idReal, zona, direccion, lat, lng, enabled: true,
                        createdAt: serverTimestamp(), updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                    });
                    Swal.fire("¡Guardado!", "La agencia ha sido registrada con éxito.", "success");
                }
                closeModal();
            } catch (err) {
                Swal.fire("Error", "Ocurrió un error al guardar: " + err.message, "error");
            } finally {
                btnSaveSingle.disabled = false;
                btnSaveSingleIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-floppy-disk";
                btnSaveSingleText.textContent = "Guardar Agencia";
            }
        });

        dropZone.addEventListener("click", () => excelFileInput.click());
        dropZone.addEventListener("dragover", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.add("border-brand-500", "bg-slate-900"); });
        ["dragleave", "dragend"].forEach(t => dropZone.addEventListener(t, () => dropZone.classList.remove("border-brand-500", "bg-slate-900")));
        dropZone.addEventListener("drop", (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            dropZone.classList.remove("border-brand-500", "bg-slate-900");
            if (e.dataTransfer.files.length > 0) handleFileSelection(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
        });
        excelFileInput.addEventListener("change", (e) => { if (e.target.files.length > 0) handleFileSelection(e.target.files[0]); });
        btnClearFile.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedFile = null;
            excelFileInput.value = "";
            fileNameDisplay.classList.add("hidden");
            fileNameDisplay.classList.remove("flex");
        });

        function handleFileSelection(file) {
            if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".xlsx") && !file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".xls")) {
                Swal.fire("Archivo Inválido", "Selecciona un archivo con extensión .xlsx o .xls", "error");
                return;
            }
            selectedFile = file;
            fileNameText.textContent = file.name;
            fileNameDisplay.classList.remove("hidden");
            fileNameDisplay.classList.add("flex");
        }

        btnProcessData.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const selectedZone = selectZona.value.trim().toUpperCase();
            if (activeTab === "excel") {
                if (!selectedFile) {
                    Swal.fire("Atención", "Selecciona un archivo Excel primero.", "warning");
                    return;
                }
                processExcelFile(selectedFile, selectedZone);
            } else {
                const rawText = rawTextarea.value.trim();
                if (!rawText) {
                    Swal.fire("Atención", "Escribe o pega los datos en el área de texto.", "warning");
                    return;
                }
                processTextareaContent(rawText, selectedZone);
            }
        });

        function processExcelFile(file, globalZone) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (e) => {
                try {
                    const data = new Uint8Array(e.target.result);
                    const workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: "array" });
                    const worksheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]];
                    const rawData = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(worksheet, { defval: "" });
                    parsedAgencies = rawData.map(row => normalizeRow(row, globalZone)).filter(a => a.idReal !== "");
                    validateAndShowPreview();
                } catch (err) {
                    Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudo leer el archivo Excel.", "error");
                }
            };
            reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
        }

        function normalizeRow(row, globalZone) {
            const lower = {};
            Object.keys(row).forEach(k => { lower[String(k).trim().toLowerCase()] = row[k]; });
            const idReal = String(lower["idreal"] ?? lower["id real"] ?? lower["id"] ?? lower["codigo"] ?? "").trim();
            const rowZone = String(lower["zona"] ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
            const zona = globalZone || rowZone || "GENERAL";
            const direccion = String(lower["direccion"] ?? lower["dirección"] ?? lower["address"] ?? "").trim();
            const lat = parseFloat(lower["lat"] ?? lower["latitud"] ?? 0) || 0;
            const lng = parseFloat(lower["lng"] ?? lower["longitud"] ?? lower["lon"] ?? 0) || 0;
            return { idReal, zona, direccion, lat, lng };
        }

        function processTextareaContent(text, globalZone) {
            const lines = text.split("\n");
            parsedAgencies = [];
            lines.forEach((line) => {
                if (!line.trim()) return;
                const delimiter = line.includes("\t") ? "\t" : ",";
                const parts = line.split(delimiter).map(p => p.trim());
                if (parts.length === 0 || !parts[0]) return;
                const first = parts[0].toLowerCase();
                if (first === "idreal" || first === "id" || first === "codigo") return;
                let idReal, zona, direccion, lat, lng;
                if (parts.length >= 5) {
                    [idReal, zona, direccion, lat, lng] = parts;
                } else {
                    idReal = parts[0];
                    direccion = parts[1] || "";
                    lat = parts[2] || 0;
                    lng = parts[3] || 0;
                    zona = "";
                }
                zona = (globalZone || String(zona || "").toUpperCase().trim()) || "GENERAL";
                parsedAgencies.push({
                    idReal: String(idReal).trim(),
                    zona,
                    direccion: String(direccion || "").trim(),
                    lat: parseFloat(lat) || 0,
                    lng: parseFloat(lng) || 0
                });
            });
            validateAndShowPreview();
        }

        function validateAndShowPreview() {
            if (parsedAgencies.length === 0) {
                Swal.fire("Sin Registros", "No se encontraron filas válidas.", "error");
                btnSaveAgencies.disabled = true;
                previewCard.classList.add("hidden");
                return;
            }
            const dedupMap = new Map();
            parsedAgencies.forEach(a => dedupMap.set(a.idReal, a));
            const beforeDedup = parsedAgencies.length;
            parsedAgencies = Array.from(dedupMap.values());
            const dupsRemoved = beforeDedup - parsedAgencies.length;

            renderPreviewTable(parsedAgencies);
            btnSaveAgencies.disabled = false;

            Swal.fire({
                title: "Previsualización Lista",
                html: `Se cargaron <b>${parsedAgencies.length}</b> registros únicos.` +
                    (dupsRemoved > 0 ? `<br><small class="text-amber-400">(${dupsRemoved} duplicados internos eliminados)</small>` : ""),
                icon: "success",
                timer: 2500,
                showConfirmButton: false
            });
        }

        function renderPreviewTable(data) {
            previewTableBody.innerHTML = "";
            recordCountBadge.textContent = `${data.length} registros`;
            const LIMIT = 200;
            data.slice(0, LIMIT).forEach((item, index) => {
                const tr = document.createElement("tr");
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition-colors";

                tr.appendChild(createCell(String(index + 1), "py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-500"));

                const tdId = document.createElement("td");
                tdId.className = "py-3 px-4";
                const spanId = document.createElement("span");
                spanId.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-500/10 text-brand-400 font-mono font-bold border border-brand-500/20";
                spanId.textContent = item.idReal;
                tdId.appendChild(spanId);
                tr.appendChild(tdId);

                const tdZona = document.createElement("td");
                tdZona.className = "py-3 px-4";
                const spanZona = document.createElement("span");
                spanZona.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 font-medium text-[10px]";
                spanZona.textContent = item.zona;
                tdZona.appendChild(spanZona);
                tr.appendChild(tdZona);

                tr.appendChild(createCell(item.direccion || "—", "py-3 px-4 font-medium text-slate-200"));
                tr.appendChild(createCell(String(item.lat), "py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-400"));
                tr.appendChild(createCell(String(item.lng), "py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-400"));
                tr.appendChild(createCell("Nueva", "py-3 px-4 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-400"));

                previewTableBody.appendChild(tr);
            });
            previewCard.classList.remove("hidden");
        }

        function createCell(text, className) {
            const td = document.createElement("td");
            td.className = className;
            td.textContent = text;
            return td;
        }

        btnSaveAgencies.addEventListener("click", async () => {
            if (parsedAgencies.length === 0) return;
            const strategy = importStrategy.value;

            const confirmResult = await Swal.fire({
                title: "¿Guardar Agencias?",
                html: `Se procesarán <b>${parsedAgencies.length}</b> registros.`,
                icon: "question",
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: "#6366f1",
                cancelButtonColor: "#475569",
                confirmButtonText: "Sí, guardar ahora",
                cancelButtonText: "Cancelar"
            });

            if (!confirmResult.isConfirmed) return;

            btnSaveAgencies.disabled = true;
            btnProcessData.disabled = true;
            progressContainer.classList.remove("hidden");
            progressLabel.textContent = "Consultando registros existentes...";
            updateProgress(5);

            try {
                const allIds = parsedAgencies.map(a => a.idReal);
                const existingMap = await fetchExistingIdMap(allIds);

                progressLabel.textContent = "Guardando en Firestore...";
                updateProgress(15);

                const agenciasRef = collection(db, "agencias");
                let processed = 0, created = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;
                let batch = writeBatch(db);
                let opCount = 0;

                const commitIfNeeded = async (force = false) => {
                    if (opCount >= 450 || (force && opCount > 0)) {
                        await batch.commit();
                        batch = writeBatch(db);
                        opCount = 0;
                    }
                };

                for (const agency of parsedAgencies) {
                    const existingDocId = existingMap.get(agency.idReal);
                    if (existingDocId) {
                        if (strategy === "skip") {
                            skipped++;
                        } else {
                            const ref = doc(db, "agencias", existingDocId);
                            batch.update(ref, {
                                zona: agency.zona, direccion: agency.direccion,
                                lat: agency.lat, lng: agency.lng, updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                            });
                            opCount++;
                            updated++;
                        }
                    } else {
                        const newRef = doc(agenciasRef);
                        batch.set(newRef, {
                            idReal: agency.idReal, zona: agency.zona, direccion: agency.direccion,
                            lat: agency.lat, lng: agency.lng, enabled: true,
                            createdAt: serverTimestamp(), updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                        });
                        opCount++;
                        created++;
                    }
                    processed++;
                    updateProgress(15 + Math.round((processed / parsedAgencies.length) * 80));
                    await commitIfNeeded();
                }

                await commitIfNeeded(true);
                updateProgress(100);

                await Swal.fire({
                    title: "¡Proceso completado!",
                    html: `
                        <div class="text-left text-sm space-y-1">
                            <p>✅ <b>${created}</b> agencias creadas</p>
                            <p>🔄 <b>${updated}</b> agencias actualizadas</p>
                            <p>⏭️ <b>${skipped}</b> agencias omitidas</p>
                        </div>
                    `,
                    icon: "success",
                    confirmButtonColor: "#6366f1"
                });

                parsedAgencies = [];
                previewCard.classList.add("hidden");
                btnSaveAgencies.disabled = true;
                progressContainer.classList.add("hidden");
                updateProgress(0);
                await loadZones();

            } catch (error) {
                Swal.fire("Error", error.message, "error");
            } finally {
                btnSaveAgencies.disabled = false;
                btnProcessData.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        async function fetchExistingIdMap(idRealArray) {
            const unique = Array.from(new Set(idRealArray.filter(Boolean)));
            const CHUNK = 30;
            const existingMap = new Map();
            const chunks = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < unique.length; i += CHUNK) chunks.push(unique.slice(i, i + CHUNK));

            for (const chunk of chunks) {
                const q = query(collection(db, "agencias"), where("idReal", "in", chunk));
                const snap = await getDocs(q);
                snap.forEach(d => {
                    const idr = d.data().idReal;
                    if (idr !== undefined && idr !== null) existingMap.set(String(idr), d.id);
                });
            }
            return existingMap;
        }

        function updateProgress(percentage) {
            const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percentage));
            progressBar.style.width = `${clamped}%`;
            progressText.textContent = `${clamped}%`;
        }

        function escapeHtml(str) {
            return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
                "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;"
            }[c]));
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

<body class="bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 min-h-screen flex antialiased">

    <div id="sidebar-container" class="h-full flex shrink-0"></div>

    <div class="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-screen overflow-hidden">

        <!-- HEADER (no se imprime) -->
        <header class="no-print h-16 bg-slate-900/80 border-b border-slate-800/80 px-4 sm:px-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 shrink-0 backdrop-blur-md">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                <button id="openSidebarMobile" class="lg:hidden text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-2 shrink-0" aria-label="Abrir menú">
                    <i class="fa fa-bars"></i>
                </button>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                    <h1 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-100 truncate">Generador de Impresión</h1>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">Selecciona zonas y genera el reporte para imprimir</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <button id="btnPrint"
                class="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/20 transition">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-print"></i>
                <span class="hidden sm:inline">Imprimir</span>
            </button>
        </header>

        <!-- MAIN -->
        <main class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scroll p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-6 bg-slate-950">

            <!-- PANEL DE CONTROLES (no se imprime) -->
            <div class="no-print bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-5 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl">
                <h2 class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-200 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-sliders text-brand-500"></i>
                    Configuración del Reporte
                </h2>

                <!-- Modo -->
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">
                        ¿Qué deseas imprimir?
                    </label>
                    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                        <label class="cursor-pointer bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3 hover:border-brand-500/50 transition">
                            <input type="radio" name="printMode" value="all" checked class="accent-brand-500 w-4 h-4">
                            <div>
                                <div class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-100">Todas las agencias</div>
                                <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Reporte completo</div>
                            </div>
                        </label>
                        <label class="cursor-pointer bg-slate-950 border-2 border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3 hover:border-brand-500/50 transition">
                            <input type="radio" name="printMode" value="zones" class="accent-brand-500 w-4 h-4">
                            <div>
                                <div class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-100">Solo zonas específicas</div>
                                <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Filtra por una o varias zonas</div>
                            </div>
                        </label>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Selector de zonas -->
                <div id="zonesSelector" class="hidden space-y-3">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                            Zonas a incluir
                        </label>
                        <div class="flex gap-3 text-[11px] font-bold">
                            <button id="btnSelectAllZones" class="text-brand-400 hover:text-brand-300">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-check-double mr-1"></i>Seleccionar todas
                            </button>
                            <button id="btnClearZones" class="text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark mr-1"></i>Limpiar
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div id="zonesGrid"
                        class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2 bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-3 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto custom-scroll">
                        <!-- Dinámico -->
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Nombre -->
                <div>
                    <label for="printName" class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 mb-2 uppercase tracking-wider">
                        Nombre en el encabezado (opcional)
                    </label>
                    <input type="text" id="printName" maxlength="80"
                        placeholder="Ej. Juan Pérez"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                    <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1.5">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info mr-1"></i>
                        Se imprimirá sobre la línea del encabezado. También puedes escribirlo a mano después de imprimir.
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- Footer controles -->
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-building text-brand-400 mr-1.5"></i>
                        Agencias a imprimir:
                        <span id="countPrint" class="font-black text-white">0</span>
                    </p>
                    <button id="btnPreviewRefresh"
                        class="text-xs font-bold text-brand-400 hover:text-brand-300 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-brand-500/10">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-right"></i> Refrescar vista previa

            <!-- ÁREA IMPRIMIBLE -->
            <div id="printArea" class="rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-10 shadow-2xl">

                <!-- ENCABEZADO -->
                <div style="border: 1px solid #999; border-radius: 6px; padding: 12px; margin-bottom: 12px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; gap: 12px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;">
                            <svg style="width: 32px; height: 42px; fill: #4f46e5;" viewBox="0 0 100 130" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
                                <g>
                                    <rect x="0" y="0" width="18" height="130" rx="4" />
                                    <rect x="26" y="0" width="18" height="70" rx="4" />

                            <div>
                                <div style="font-weight: 800; font-size: 15pt; line-height: 1.1;">GesagPro</div>
                                <div style="font-size: 8pt; color: #555;">Sistema de Gestión de Agencias</div>
                            </div>

                        <div style="text-align: right; font-size: 9pt;">
                            <div style="font-weight: 700;">Reporte de Agencias</div>
                            <div style="margin-top: 4px;"><b>Fecha de impresión:</b></div>
                            <div id="printDate" style="font-family: monospace; font-size: 9pt;">—</div>
                        </div>

                    <div style="margin-top: 12px; padding-top: 10px; border-top: 1px dashed #999;">
                        <div style="font-size: 10.5pt; margin-bottom: 6px;">
                            <b>Nombre:</b>
                            <span id="printNameDisplay"
                                style="display: inline-block; min-width: 280px; border-bottom: 1px solid #000; padding: 0 6px 2px;">
                            </span>
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size: 10pt;">
                            <b>Filtro aplicado:</b>
                            <span id="printFilterDisplay">Todas las agencias</span>
                            &nbsp;·&nbsp;
                            <b>Total:</b>
                            <span id="printTotalDisplay">0 agencias</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- TABLA -->
                <table id="printTable" style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 9pt;">
                    <thead style="background: #e5e7eb;">
                        <tr>
                            <th style="border: 1px solid #999; padding: 5px 6px; width: 34px;">#</th>

                        </tr>

        Generado por <b>Gesag Dev Pedro Garcia</b> · GesagPro © 2026
    </div>

    <script type="module">
        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";
        initSidebar("sidebar-container");

        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";
        import { collection, getDocs, doc, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // DOM
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const printModeRadios   = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="printMode"]');
        const zonesSelector     = document.getElementById("zonesSelector");
        const zonesGrid         = document.getElementById("zonesGrid");
        const btnSelectAllZones = document.getElementById("btnSelectAllZones");
        const btnClearZones     = document.getElementById("btnClearZones");
        const printNameInput    = document.getElementById("printName");
        const printNameDisplay  = document.getElementById("printNameDisplay");
        const countPrint        = document.getElementById("countPrint");
        const printTotalDisplay = document.getElementById("printTotalDisplay");
        const btnPrint          = document.getElementById("btnPrint");
        const btnPreviewRefresh = document.getElementById("btnPreviewRefresh");
        const printDateEl       = document.getElementById("printDate");
        const printFilterDisplay= document.getElementById("printFilterDisplay");
        const printTableBody    = document.getElementById("printTableBody");

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ESTADO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        let allAgencies  = [];
        let zonesList    = [];
        let selectedZones = new Set();

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // AUTENTICACIÓN + PERMISOS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) { window.location.replace("login.html"); return; }

            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));

                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    await Swal.fire("Cuenta no registrada", "Contacta al administrador.", "error");
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const profile = userSnap.data();

                if (profile.enabled === false) {
                    await Swal.fire("Usuario inhabilitado", "Tu acceso ha sido revocado.", "warning");
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                // ── Verificar permiso al módulo "imprimir" ──
                const isAdmin = profile.role === "admin";
                const modulos = Array.isArray(profile.modulos) ? profile.modulos : [];
                const puedeImprimir = isAdmin || modulos.includes("imprimir");

                if (!puedeImprimir) {
                    await Swal.fire({
                        icon: "error",
                        title: "Acceso denegado",
                        text: "No tienes permiso para acceder al módulo de impresión. Contacta al administrador.",
                        confirmButtonText: "Aceptar"
                    });
                    window.location.replace("index.html");
                    return;
                }

                // ── OK: inicializar ──
                printDateEl.textContent = new Date().toLocaleString("es-DO", {
                    day: "2-digit", month: "long", year: "numeric",
                    hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit"
                });

                await loadAgencies();
                buildZonesGrid();
                bindEvents();
                updatePreview();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al verificar permisos:", err);
                await Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudieron verificar tus credenciales.", "error");
                window.location.replace("login.html");
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CARGA DE AGENCIAS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        async function loadAgencies() {
            const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, "agencias"));
            allAgencies = [];
            const zonesSet = new Set();

            snap.forEach(docSnap => {
                const data = docSnap.data();
                const realId = data.idReal ?? data.idreal ?? data.codigo ?? null;
                const lat = data.lat ?? data.latitud ?? null;
                const lng = data.lng ?? data.longitud ?? null;
                const zona = data.zona ? String(data.zona).toUpperCase().trim() : "SIN ZONA";

                if (zona) zonesSet.add(zona);

                allAgencies.push({
                    id: docSnap.id,
                    idReal: realId !== null ? String(realId) : "",
                    zona,
                    direccion: String(data.direccion || "").trim(),
                    lat: lat !== null ? Number(lat) : 0,
                    lng: lng !== null ? Number(lng) : 0,
                    enabled: data.enabled !== false
                });
            });

            allAgencies.sort((a, b) =>
                a.idReal.localeCompare(b.idReal, undefined, { numeric: true })
            );

            zonesList = Array.from(zonesSet).sort();
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // GRID DE ZONAS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function buildZonesGrid() {
            zonesGrid.innerHTML = "";

            if (zonesList.length === 0) {
                zonesGrid.innerHTML = `<p class="col-span-full text-center text-xs text-slate-500 py-4">No hay zonas registradas.</p>`;
                return;
            }

            zonesList.forEach(zone => {
                const chip = document.createElement("label");
                chip.className = "zone-chip";
                chip.dataset.zone = zone;

                const cb = document.createElement("input");
                cb.type = "checkbox";
                cb.value = zone;
                cb.addEventListener("change", () => {
                    if (cb.checked) {
                        selectedZones.add(zone);
                        chip.classList.add("selected");
                    } else {
                        selectedZones.delete(zone);
                        chip.classList.remove("selected");
                    }
                    updatePreview();
                });

                const txt = document.createElement("span");
                txt.textContent = zone;

                const count = document.createElement("span");
                count.className = "ml-auto text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500";
                count.textContent = `(${allAgencies.filter(a => a.zona === zone).length})`;

                chip.appendChild(cb);
                chip.appendChild(txt);
                chip.appendChild(count);
                zonesGrid.appendChild(chip);
            });
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // EVENTOS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function bindEvents() {
            printModeRadios.forEach(r => r.addEventListener("change", () => {
                const mode = document.querySelector('input[name="printMode"]:checked').value;
                zonesSelector.classList.toggle("hidden", mode !== "zones");
                updatePreview();
            }));

            btnSelectAllZones.addEventListener("click", () => {
                zonesList.forEach(z => selectedZones.add(z));
                syncZoneCheckboxes();
                updatePreview();
            });

            btnClearZones.addEventListener("click", () => {
                selectedZones.clear();
                syncZoneCheckboxes();
                updatePreview();
            });

            printNameInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
                printNameDisplay.textContent = printNameInput.value.trim();
            });

            btnPreviewRefresh.addEventListener("click", updatePreview);

            btnPrint.addEventListener("click", () => {
                if (getFilteredAgencies().length === 0) {
                    Swal.fire("Sin datos", "No hay agencias para imprimir con los filtros seleccionados.", "warning");
                    return;
                }
                window.print();
            });
        }

        function syncZoneCheckboxes() {
            zonesGrid.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
                const should = selectedZones.has(cb.value);
                cb.checked = should;
                cb.closest(".zone-chip").classList.toggle("selected", should);
            });
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // FILTRO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function getFilteredAgencies() {
            const mode = document.querySelector('input[name="printMode"]:checked').value;

            if (mode === "all") return allAgencies;
            return allAgencies.filter(a => selectedZones.has(a.zona));
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // PREVIEW / RENDER
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function updatePreview() {
            const list = getFilteredAgencies();
            countPrint.textContent = list.length;
            printTotalDisplay.textContent = `${list.length} agencia${list.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;

            const mode = document.querySelector('input[name="printMode"]:checked').value;
            if (mode === "all") {
                printFilterDisplay.textContent = "Todas las agencias";
            } else if (selectedZones.size === 0) {
                printFilterDisplay.textContent = "Ninguna zona seleccionada";
            } else {
                printFilterDisplay.textContent = `Zonas: ${Array.from(selectedZones).sort().join(", ")}`;
            }

            printNameDisplay.textContent = printNameInput.value.trim();

            printTableBody.innerHTML = "";

            if (list.length === 0) {
                printTableBody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; padding: 30px; color: #888;">
                            No hay agencias para mostrar con los filtros seleccionados.
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                return;
            }

            list.forEach((agency, idx) => {
                const tr = document.createElement("tr");

                const coords = (agency.lat && agency.lng)
                    ? `${agency.lat.toFixed(5)}, ${agency.lng.toFixed(5)}`
                    : "—";

                const estado = agency.enabled ? "Activa" : "Inactiva";
                const estadoColor = agency.enabled ? "#059669" : "#dc2626";

                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td style="border: 1px solid #888; padding: 4px 6px; text-align: center; font-family: monospace;">${idx + 1}</td>
                    <td style="border: 1px solid #888; padding: 4px 6px; font-weight: 700;">${escapeHtml(agency.idReal || "—")}</td>
                    <td style="border: 1px solid #888; padding: 4px 6px;">${escapeHtml(agency.zona)}</td>
                    <td style="border: 1px solid #888; padding: 4px 6px;">${escapeHtml(agency.direccion || "—")}</td>
                    <td style="border: 1px solid #888; padding: 4px 6px; font-family: monospace; font-size: 8pt;">${coords}</td>
                    <td style="border: 1px solid #888; padding: 4px 6px; text-align: center; color: ${estadoColor}; font-weight: 700;">${estado}</td>
                `;
                printTableBody.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // UTILIDADES
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function escapeHtml(str) {
            return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
                "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;"
            }[c]));
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

ARCHIVO: index.html
================================================================================

    <div id="sidebar-container" class="h-full flex shrink-0"></div>

                <button id="openSidebarMobile" class="lg:hidden text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-2 text-base" aria-label="Abrir menú">
                    <i class="fa fa-bars"></i>
                </button>
                <div class="hidden sm:block">
                    <h2 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-100">Directorio General de Agencias</h2>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Monitoreo y administración global de sucursales</p>

            <div class="flex-1 max-w-md">
                <div class="relative">
                    <span class="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-slate-500 text-xs">
                        <i class="fa fa-magnifying-glass"></i>

                    <input id="searchInput" type="text"
                        placeholder="Buscar por código, zona o dirección..."

        <main class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-6 custom-scroll bg-slate-950">

            <!-- KPIs -->

                        <p class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Agencias Activas</p>
