                <h3 class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Listado Registrado</h3>
                <a href="configuracion.html" data-admin-only
                    class="bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-500 text-white px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-md active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fa fa-plus text-xs"></i>
                    <span>Nueva Agencia</span>
                </a>
            </div>

            <!-- LOADING -->
            <div id="loading" class="text-center py-16">
                <i class="fa fa-circle-notch animate-spin text-3xl text-brand-500 mb-3"></i>
                <p class="text-slate-400 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Cargando base de datos Firestore...</p>
            </div>

            <!-- GRID -->
            <div id="agenciesList" class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4 pb-12"></div>

        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- MODAL EDITAR AGENCIA -->
    <div id="editModal" class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-[9998] flex items-center justify-center hidden p-4">
        <div class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-lg space-y-4 shadow-2xl">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i class="fa fa-pen-to-square text-brand-400"></i>
                    Editar Agencia
                </h3>
                <button id="btnCloseEditModal" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white" aria-label="Cerrar">
                    <i class="fa fa-xmark"></i>
                </button>
            </div>

            <form id="editForm" class="space-y-3 text-xs" novalidate>
                <input type="hidden" id="editDocId">

                <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">ID Real</label>
                    <input type="text" id="editIdReal" required
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Zona</label>
                    <input type="text" id="editZona" list="zonesDatalist" required
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500 font-bold uppercase">
                    <datalist id="zonesDatalist"></datalist>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Dirección</label>
                    <input type="text" id="editDireccion"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                </div>

                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Latitud</label>
                        <input type="number" step="any" id="editLat"
                            class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Longitud</label>
                        <input type="number" step="any" id="editLng"
                            class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
                    <button type="button" id="btnCancelEdit"
                        class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold rounded-xl">
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button type="submit" id="btnSaveEdit"
                        class="px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-500 disabled:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2">
                        <i class="fa fa-floppy-disk" id="btnSaveEditIcon"></i>
                        <span id="btnSaveEditText">Guardar Cambios</span>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- SCRIPT PRINCIPAL -->
    <script type="module">
    import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";
    initSidebar("sidebar-container");

        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

        import {
            collection,
            doc,
            getDoc,
            getDocs,
            updateDoc,
            deleteDoc,
            serverTimestamp
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

        import {
            onAuthStateChanged,
            signOut
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // DOM
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const bootOverlay     = document.getElementById("bootOverlay");
        const agenciesList    = document.getElementById("agenciesList");
        const searchInput     = document.getElementById("searchInput");
        const totalBadge      = document.getElementById("totalBadge");
        const activeBadge     = document.getElementById("activeBadge");
        const inactiveBadge   = document.getElementById("inactiveBadge");
        const loading         = document.getElementById("loading");
        const btnLogout       = document.getElementById("btnLogout");
        const userEmailSpan   = document.getElementById("userEmail");
        const userRoleSpan    = document.getElementById("userRole");

        // Modal editar
        const editModal       = document.getElementById("editModal");
        const editForm        = document.getElementById("editForm");
        const editDocId       = document.getElementById("editDocId");
        const editIdReal      = document.getElementById("editIdReal");
        const editZona        = document.getElementById("editZona");
        const editDireccion   = document.getElementById("editDireccion");
        const editLat         = document.getElementById("editLat");
        const editLng         = document.getElementById("editLng");
        const zonesDatalist   = document.getElementById("zonesDatalist");
        const btnSaveEdit     = document.getElementById("btnSaveEdit");
        const btnSaveEditText = document.getElementById("btnSaveEditText");
        const btnSaveEditIcon = document.getElementById("btnSaveEditIcon");

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ESTADO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        let allAgencies   = [];
        let isAdmin       = false;
        let currentUser   = null;
        let currentStatusFilter = "ALL";

        // Referencias a las tarjetas de filtro KPI
        const filterCardTotal = document.getElementById("filterCardTotal");
        const filterCardActive = document.getElementById("filterCardActive");
        const filterCardInactive = document.getElementById("filterCardInactive");


        // Eventos para filtrar al hacer clic en las tarjetas de KPIs
        filterCardTotal.addEventListener("click", () => {
            currentStatusFilter = "ALL";
            updateFilterCardStyles();
            applyFilters();
        });

        filterCardActive.addEventListener("click", () => {
            currentStatusFilter = "ACTIVE";
            updateFilterCardStyles();
            applyFilters();
        });

        filterCardInactive.addEventListener("click", () => {
            currentStatusFilter = "INACTIVE";
            updateFilterCardStyles();
            applyFilters();
        });

        function updateFilterCardStyles() {
            filterCardTotal.className = `bg-slate-900/60 p-4 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all border ${currentStatusFilter === 'ALL' ? 'border-brand-500 bg-slate-900' : 'border-slate-800/80'}`;
            filterCardActive.className = `bg-slate-900/60 p-4 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all border ${currentStatusFilter === 'ACTIVE' ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-slate-900' : 'border-slate-800/80'}`;
            filterCardInactive.className = `bg-slate-900/60 p-4 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all border ${currentStatusFilter === 'INACTIVE' ? 'border-rose-500 bg-slate-900' : 'border-slate-800/80'}`;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // AUTENTICACIÓN + VERIFICACIÓN DE ROL
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }

            currentUser = user;
            userEmailSpan.innerText = user.email;

            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));

                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Tu cuenta no está registrada. Contacta al administrador.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const userData = userSnap.data();

                if (userData.enabled === false) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Tu usuario ha sido inhabilitado.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                isAdmin = userData.role === "admin";
                userRoleSpan.innerText = isAdmin ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador";
                userRoleSpan.className = isAdmin
                    ? "text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wider"
                    : "text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider";

                // Ocultar elementos solo-admin para viewers
                if (!isAdmin) {
                    document.querySelectorAll("[data-admin-only]").forEach(el => el.remove());
                }

                // Ocultar overlay inicial
                bootOverlay.classList.add("hidden");

                // Cargar datos
                await loadAgencies();

            } catch (err) {
                Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Error al verificar tus credenciales.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                window.location.replace("login.html");
            }
        });

        // Logout
        
btnLogout.addEventListener("click", async () => {
    const result = await Swal.fire({
        title: "¿Cerrar sesión?",
        text: "¿Seguro que deseas cerrar sesión?",
        icon: "question",
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: "#d33",
        cancelButtonColor: "#6c757d",
        confirmButtonText: "Sí, cerrar sesión",
        cancelButtonText: "Cancelar",
        reverseButtons: true
    });

    if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

    try {
        await signOut(auth);

        await Swal.fire({
            title: "¡Sesión cerrada!",
            text: "Has cerrado sesión correctamente.",
            icon: "success",
            timer: 1200,
            showConfirmButton: false,
            allowOutsideClick: false
        });

        window.location.replace("login.html");
    } catch (error) {
        Swal.fire({
            title: "Error",
            text: "No se pudo cerrar la sesión. Inténtalo nuevamente.",
            icon: "error",
            confirmButtonText: "Aceptar"
        });
    }
});


        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CARGA DE AGENCIAS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        async function loadAgencies() {
            try {
                loading.style.display = "block";
                agenciesList.innerHTML = "";

                const snapshot = await getDocs(collection(db, "agencias"));
                allAgencies = [];

                snapshot.forEach((docSnap) => {
                    allAgencies.push({ id: docSnap.id, ...docSnap.data() });
                });

                // Ordenar por idReal ascendente
                allAgencies.sort((a, b) => {
                    const aKey = String(a.idReal ?? "").toLowerCase();
                    const bKey = String(b.idReal ?? "").toLowerCase();
                    return aKey.localeCompare(bKey, undefined, { numeric: true });
                });

                populateZonesDatalist();
                renderAgencies(allAgencies);

            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Error Firestore:", error);
                const denied = error.code === "permission-denied";
                agenciesList.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="col-span-full text-center py-12 bg-rose-500/5 rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-400">
                        <i class="fa fa-triangle-exclamation text-3xl mb-2"></i>
                        <p class="font-bold text-sm">${denied ? "Permisos insuficientes para leer agencias." : "Error de conexión con la base de datos."}</p>
                        <p class="text-[11px] text-rose-300/70 mt-1">Revisa las reglas de Firestore o tu conexión.</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            } finally {
                loading.style.display = "none";
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // RENDER DE LISTA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function renderAgencies(agencies) {
            agenciesList.innerHTML = "";

            // KPIs siempre sobre el total, no sobre lo filtrado
            const total = allAgencies.length;
            const activeCount = allAgencies.filter(a => a.enabled !== false).length;
            totalBadge.innerText = total;
            activeBadge.innerText = activeCount;
            inactiveBadge.innerText = total - activeCount;

            if (agencies.length === 0) {
                agenciesList.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="col-span-full text-center py-16 bg-slate-900/40 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-800">
                        <i class="fa fa-store-slash text-3xl text-slate-600 mb-3"></i>
                        <p class="text-slate-300 font-bold text-sm">No se encontraron agencias</p>
                        <p class="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">Intenta con otro término de búsqueda.</p>
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }

            agencies.forEach((agency) => {
                agenciesList.appendChild(createAgencyCard(agency));
            });
        }

        function createAgencyCard(agency) {
            const isEnabled = agency.enabled !== false;
            const name = agency.idReal || "Sin ID";
            const zone = agency.zona || "Sin Zona";
            const initial = zone.length >= 2 ? zone.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase() : "AG";
            const lat = agency.lat;
            const lng = agency.lng;
            const address = agency.direccion || "";

            let mapUrl = null;
            if (lat && lng) {
                mapUrl = `https://www.google.com/maps?q=${lat},${lng}`;
            } else if (address) {
                mapUrl = `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(address + " " + name)}`;
            }

            const card = document.createElement("div");
            card.className = `group relative p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 ${
                !isEnabled
                    ? "bg-slate-900/30 border-slate-800/50 opacity-60"
                    : "bg-slate-900/70 border-slate-800/80 hover:border-brand-500/40 hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-brand-500/5 hover:-translate-y-1"
            }`;

            card.innerHTML = `
                <div>
                    <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                            <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-black text-xs tracking-wider shrink-0 ${
                                isEnabled
                                    ? "bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-600 to-indigo-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-brand-500/20"
                                    : "bg-slate-800 text-slate-500"
                            }">
                                ${escapeHtml(initial)}
                            </div>
                            <div class="min-w-0">
                                <h4 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-100 truncate group-hover:text-brand-400 transition-colors">
                                    ${escapeHtml(String(name))}
                                </h4>
                                <span class="inline-block text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-slate-800/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-slate-700/50 mt-0.5">
                                    ${escapeHtml(zone)}
                                </span>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[9px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider shrink-0 ${
                            isEnabled
                                ? "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20"
                                : "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/20"
                        }">
                            <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${isEnabled ? "bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" : "bg-rose-400"}"></span>
                            ${isEnabled ? "Activa" : "Inactiva"}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    ${address || agency.telefono ? `
                        <div class="space-y-1.5 pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-800/60 text-xs text-slate-400">
                            ${address ? `
                                <p class="flex items-center gap-2 truncate text-[11px]">
                                    <i class="fa fa-location-dot text-brand-400 w-3.5 text-center shrink-0"></i>
                                    <span class="truncate">${escapeHtml(address)}</span>
                                </p>
                            ` : ""}
                            ${agency.telefono ? `
                                <p class="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px]">
                                    <i class="fa fa-phone text-brand-400 w-3.5 text-center shrink-0"></i>
                                    <span>${escapeHtml(String(agency.telefono))}</span>
                                </p>
                            ` : ""}
                        </div>
                    ` : ""}
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-500 truncate" title="${escapeHtml(agency.id)}">
                        ID: ${escapeHtml(agency.id.substring(0, 10))}…
                    </span>

                    <div class="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                        ${mapUrl ? `
                            <a href="${mapUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"
                               class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-brand-600 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-all text-xs"
                               title="Ver en Mapa">
                                <i class="fa fa-map-location-dot"></i>
                            </a>
                        ` : ""}
                        ${isAdmin ? `
                            <button data-action="edit" data-id="${escapeHtml(agency.id)}"
                                class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center transition-all text-xs"
                                title="Editar">
                                <i class="fa fa-pen"></i>
                            </button>
                            <button data-action="toggle" data-id="${escapeHtml(agency.id)}"
                                class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center transition-all text-xs ${
                                    isEnabled
                                        ? "bg-slate-800 text-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500/20"
                                        : "bg-slate-800 text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-500/20"
                                }"
                                title="${isEnabled ? "Inhabilitar" : "Habilitar"}">
                                <i class="fa ${isEnabled ? "fa-ban" : "fa-check"}"></i>
                            </button>
                            <button data-action="delete" data-id="${escapeHtml(agency.id)}"
                                class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center transition-all text-xs"
                                title="Eliminar">
                                <i class="fa fa-trash-can"></i>
                            </button>
                        ` : ""}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;

            return card;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // DELEGACIÓN DE EVENTOS (Edit / Toggle / Delete)
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        agenciesList.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("button[data-action]");
            if (!btn) return;

            const action = btn.dataset.action;
            const id     = btn.dataset.id;
            const agency = allAgencies.find(a => a.id === id);
            if (!agency) return;

            if (action === "edit")   openEditModal(agency);
            if (action === "toggle") toggleAgencyStatus(agency);
            if (action === "delete") deleteAgency(agency);
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // EDITAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function openEditModal(agency) {
            editDocId.value     = agency.id;
            editIdReal.value    = agency.idReal || "";
            editZona.value      = agency.zona || "";
            editDireccion.value = agency.direccion || "";
            editLat.value       = agency.lat ?? "";
            editLng.value       = agency.lng ?? "";
            editModal.classList.remove("hidden");
            setTimeout(() => editIdReal.focus(), 50);
        }

        function closeEditModal() {
            editModal.classList.add("hidden");
            editForm.reset();
            editDocId.value = "";
        }

        document.getElementById("btnCloseEditModal").addEventListener("click", closeEditModal);
        document.getElementById("btnCancelEdit").addEventListener("click", closeEditModal);
        editModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            if (e.target === editModal) closeEditModal();
        });
        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape" && !editModal.classList.contains("hidden")) closeEditModal();
        });

        editForm.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (btnSaveEdit.disabled) return;

            const id = editDocId.value;
            if (!id) return;

            const payload = {
                idReal:    editIdReal.value.trim(),
                zona:      editZona.value.trim().toUpperCase(),
                direccion: editDireccion.value.trim(),
                lat:       parseFloat(editLat.value) || 0,
                lng:       parseFloat(editLng.value) || 0,
                updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
            };

            if (!payload.idReal || !payload.zona) {
                Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'warning',
                        title: 'warning!',
                        text: 'ID Real y Zona son obligatorios.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                return;
            }

            btnSaveEdit.disabled = true;
            btnSaveEditText.textContent = "Guardando...";
            btnSaveEditIcon.className = "fa fa-circle-notch fa-spin";

            try {
                await updateDoc(doc(db, "agencias", id), payload);

                // Actualizar array local
                const idx = allAgencies.findIndex(a => a.id === id);
                if (idx !== -1) {
                    allAgencies[idx] = { ...allAgencies[idx], ...payload };
                }

                closeEditModal();
                applyFilters();
            } catch (err) {
                Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'No se pudieron guardar los cambios.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
            } finally {
                btnSaveEdit.disabled = false;
                btnSaveEditText.textContent = "Guardar Cambios";
                btnSaveEditIcon.className = "fa fa-floppy-disk";
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // HABILITAR / INHABILITAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────

        async function toggleAgencyStatus(agency) {
    const isEnabled = agency.enabled !== false;
    const action = isEnabled ? "inhabilitar" : "habilitar";
    const label = agency.idReal || agency.id;

    const result = await Swal.fire({
        title: `¿${isEnabled ? "Inhabilitar" : "Habilitar"} agencia?`,
        html: `¿Seguro que deseas ${action} la agencia <strong>"${label}"</strong>?`,
        icon: 'question',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: isEnabled ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a',
        cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
        confirmButtonText: isEnabled
            ? 'Sí, inhabilitar'
            : 'Sí, habilitar',
        cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar',
        reverseButtons: true,
        focusCancel: true
    });

    if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

    try {
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Actualizando estado...',
            text: 'Por favor, espera.',
            allowOutsideClick: false,
            allowEscapeKey: false,
            didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading()
        });

        const newStatus = !isEnabled;

        await updateDoc(doc(db, "agencias", agency.id), {
            enabled: newStatus,
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
        });

        const idx = allAgencies.findIndex(a => a.id === agency.id);

        if (idx !== -1) {
            allAgencies[idx].enabled = newStatus;
        }

        applyFilters();

        await Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: newStatus ? '¡Agencia habilitada!' : '¡Agencia inhabilitada!',
            text: newStatus
                ? `La agencia "${label}" está habilitada nuevamente.`
                : `La agencia "${label}" ha sido inhabilitada correctamente.`,
            confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb',
            timer: 2500,
            timerProgressBar: true
        });

    } catch (err) {
        console.error('Error al cambiar el estado de la agencia:', err);

        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: '¡Error!',
            text: 'No se pudo cambiar el estado de la agencia. Inténtalo nuevamente.',
            confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
        });
    }
}


        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ELIMINAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
       
async function deleteAgency(agency) {
    const label = agency.idReal || agency.id;

    const result = await Swal.fire({
        title: '¿Eliminar agencia?',
        html: `¿Deseas eliminar definitivamente la agencia <strong>"${label}"</strong>?<br><br>
               <span style="color: #dc2626;">Esta acción no se puede deshacer.</span>`,
        icon: 'warning',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#dc2626',
        cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
        confirmButtonText: '<i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Sí, eliminar',
        cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar',
        reverseButtons: true,
        focusCancel: true
    });

    if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

    try {
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Eliminando...',
            text: 'Por favor, espera.',
            allowOutsideClick: false,
            allowEscapeKey: false,
            didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading()
        });

        await deleteDoc(doc(db, "agencias", agency.id));

        allAgencies = allAgencies.filter(a => a.id !== agency.id);
        applyFilters();

        await Swal.fire({
            icon: 'success',
            title: '¡Agencia eliminada!',
            text: `La agencia "${label}" se eliminó correctamente.`,
            confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb',
            timer: 2500,
            timerProgressBar: true
        });

    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error al eliminar la agencia:", err);

        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: '¡Error!',
            text: 'No se pudo eliminar la agencia. Inténtalo nuevamente.',
            confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
        });
    }
}


        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // BÚSQUEDA CON DEBOUNCE
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        let searchTimer = null;
        searchInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
            clearTimeout(searchTimer);
            searchTimer = setTimeout(applyFilters, 220);
        });

        function applyFilters() {
            const query = searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();

            const filtered = allAgencies.filter((agency) => {
                const isEnabled = agency.enabled !== false;

                // 1. Filtro por estado (Activas / Inactivas)
                if (currentStatusFilter === "ACTIVE" && !isEnabled) return false;
                if (currentStatusFilter === "INACTIVE" && isEnabled) return false;

                // 2. Filtro por texto de búsqueda
                if (!query) return true;

                const code    = String(agency.idReal ?? agency.id ?? "").toLowerCase();
                const address = String(agency.direccion ?? "").toLowerCase();
                const zone    = String(agency.zona ?? "").toLowerCase();

                return code.includes(query) || address.includes(query) || zone.includes(query);
            });

            renderAgencies(filtered);
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // DATALIST DE ZONAS PARA EL MODAL
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function populateZonesDatalist() {
            const zones = new Set();
            allAgencies.forEach(a => {
                if (a.zona) zones.add(String(a.zona).toUpperCase().trim());
            });

            zonesDatalist.innerHTML = "";
            Array.from(zones).sort().forEach(z => {
                const opt = document.createElement("option");
                opt.value = z;
                zonesDatalist.appendChild(opt);
            });
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // UTILIDAD: ESCAPE HTML (anti-XSS)
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function escapeHtml(str) {
            return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
                "&": "&amp;",
                "<": "&lt;",
                ">": "&gt;",
                '"': "&quot;",
                "'": "&#39;"
            }[c]));
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">

<link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="./img/favicon.svg">

    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

<body class="bg-slate-950 min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-4 text-slate-100 selection:bg-indigo-500 selection:text-white">

    <!-- CONTENEDOR PRINCIPAL -->

        <div class="absolute -bottom-16 -left-16 w-32 h-32 bg-blue-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none"></div>

        <!-- ENCABEZADO -->
        <div class="text-center mb-8 relative">
            <div class="w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 p-3 shadow-inner transition-transform hover:scale-105">
                <svg class="w-10 h-14 text-white fill-current" viewBox="0 0 100 130" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
                    <g>
                        <!-- P -->
                        <rect x="0" y="0" width="18" height="130" rx="4" />
                        <rect x="26" y="0" width="18" height="70" rx="4" />
                        <!-- G -->
                        <rect x="52" y="0" width="18" height="130" rx="4" />
                        <rect x="78" y="0" width="18" height="38" rx="4" />
                        <rect x="78" y="68" width="18" height="62" rx="4" />

            <h1 class="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">Bienvenido</h1>

        </div>

        <!-- FORMULARIO -->

            <div>
                <label for="email" class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                    Correo Electrónico
                </label>
                <div class="relative">
                    <i class="fa fa-envelope absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-500 text-xs pointer-events-none"></i>
                    <input type="email" id="email" name="email" required
                        autocomplete="email" spellcheck="false"
                        placeholder="usuario@app.com"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/70 text-white pl-9 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all placeholder:text-slate-600">
                </div>

            <div>
                <label for="password" class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                    Contraseña
                </label>
                <div class="relative">
                    <i class="fa fa-key absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-500 text-xs pointer-events-none"></i>
                    <input type="password" id="password" name="password" required
                        autocomplete="current-password"
                        placeholder="••••••••"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/70 text-white pl-9 pr-10 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-indigo-500 transition-all placeholder:text-slate-600">
                    <button type="button" id="togglePassword"
                        aria-label="Mostrar u ocultar contraseña"
                        class="absolute right-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300 transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:text-indigo-400">
                        <i class="fa fa-eye text-xs" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                    </button>

                <button type="button" id="btnForgotPassword"
                    class="text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 transition-colors">
                    ¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?
                </button>
            </div>

            <!-- MENSAJE DE ERROR O ALERTA -->

                class="hidden p-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-center border transition-all"></div>

            <!-- BOTÓN DE SUBMIT -->
            <button type="submit" id="btnLogin"
                class="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-blue-600 hover:from-indigo-500 hover:to-blue-500 disabled:from-slate-700 disabled:to-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/25 active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                <span id="btnText">Iniciar Sesión</span>
                <i id="btnIcon" class="fa fa-arrow-right text-xs" aria-hidden="true"></i>
            </button>

    <!-- SCRIPT DE AUTENTICACIÓN FIREBASE -->
    <script type="module">
        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";
        import {
            signInWithEmailAndPassword,
            signOut,
            onAuthStateChanged,
            sendPasswordResetEmail
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";
        import { doc, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

        const form = document.getElementById("loginForm");
        const errorMsg = document.getElementById("errorMsg");
        const emailInput = document.getElementById("email");
        const passwordInput = document.getElementById("password");
        const togglePassword = document.getElementById("togglePassword");
        const btnLogin = document.getElementById("btnLogin");
        const btnText = document.getElementById("btnText");
        const btnIcon = document.getElementById("btnIcon");
        const btnForgotPassword = document.getElementById("btnForgotPassword");

        // Bandera para evitar que onAuthStateChanged redirija mientras estamos iniciando sesión
        let isLoggingIn = false;

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // REDIRECCIÓN AUTOMÁTICA SI YA HAY SESIÓN ACTIVA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
            if (user && !isLoggingIn) {
                window.location.replace("index.html");
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // TOGGLE DE VISIBILIDAD DE CONTRASEÑA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        togglePassword.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const isPassword = passwordInput.type === "password";
            passwordInput.type = isPassword ? "text" : "password";
            togglePassword.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-${isPassword ? "eye-slash" : "eye"} text-xs" aria-hidden="true"></i>`;
            passwordInput.focus();
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // OLVIDÉ MI CONTRASEÑA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        
        btnForgotPassword.addEventListener("click", async () => {
            await Swal.fire({
                icon: "info",
                title: "¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?",
                text: "Por favor, comunícate con el administrador del sistema para recuperar tu acceso.",
                confirmButtonText: "Entendido",
                confirmButtonColor: "#2563eb"
            });
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // MANEJO DEL LOGIN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        form.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (btnLogin.disabled) return; // protección anti doble-submit

            showError("", false);
            setLoading(true);
            isLoggingIn = true;

            const email = emailInput.value.trim();
            const password = passwordInput.value;

            try {
                // 1. Autenticar
                const userCredential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
                const uid = userCredential.user.uid;

                // 2. Verificar doc en Firestore (OBLIGATORIO)
                const userDocRef = doc(db, "users", uid);
                const userDocSnap = await getDoc(userDocRef);

                if (!userDocSnap.exists()) {
                    await signOut(auth);
                    isLoggingIn = false;
                    setLoading(false);
                    showError(
                        "Tu cuenta no está registrada en el sistema. Contacta al administrador.",
                        true, "error"
                    );
                    return;
                }

                const userData = userDocSnap.data();

                // 3. Verificar estado habilitado
                if (userData.enabled === false) {
                    await signOut(auth);
                    isLoggingIn = false;
                    setLoading(false);
                    showError(
                        "Tu usuario ha sido inhabilitado. Contacta al administrador.",
                        true, "warning"
                    );
                    return;
                }

                // 4. Todo OK → redirigir
                window.location.replace("index.html");

            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Error en login:", error);
                isLoggingIn = false;
                setLoading(false);
                showError(getFriendlyError(error), true, "error");
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // UTILIDADES
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────

        /**
         * Traduce los códigos de Firebase Auth a mensajes claros en español.
         */
        function getFriendlyError(error) {
            switch (error.code) {
                case "auth/invalid-email":
                    return "El formato del correo no es válido.";
                case "auth/user-not-found":
                    return "No existe una cuenta con ese correo.";
                case "auth/wrong-password":
                case "auth/invalid-credential":
                    return "Correo o contraseña incorrectos.";
                case "auth/too-many-requests":
                    return "Demasiados intentos fallidos. Espera unos minutos e intenta de nuevo.";
                case "auth/network-request-failed":
                    return "Sin conexión. Verifica tu red e intenta de nuevo.";
                case "auth/user-disabled":
                    return "Esta cuenta ha sido deshabilitada por un administrador.";
                default:
                    return "No se pudo iniciar sesión. Verifica tus datos e intenta de nuevo.";
            }
        }

        /**
         * Muestra u oculta el mensaje de alerta.
         * @param {string} msg - Texto a mostrar
         * @param {boolean} visible - Si se muestra o no
         * @param {"error"|"warning"|"success"} type - Estilo visual
         */
        function showError(msg, visible, type = "error") {
            const styles = {
                error:   "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/20",
                warning: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/20",
                success: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/20",
            };
            errorMsg.innerText = msg;
            errorMsg.className = `p-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-center border transition-all ${styles[type] || styles.error}`;
            errorMsg.classList.toggle("hidden", !visible);
        }

        /**
         * Activa/desactiva el estado de carga del botón.
         */
        function setLoading(isLoading) {
            btnLogin.disabled = isLoading;
            btnText.innerText = isLoading ? "Verificando..." : "Iniciar Sesión";
            btnIcon.className = isLoading
                ? "fa fa-circle-notch animate-spin text-xs"
                : "fa fa-arrow-right text-xs";
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                    Editar Agencia
                </h3>
                <button id="btnCloseModal" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white" aria-label="Cerrar">
                    <i class="fa fa-xmark"></i>
                </button>
            </div>

            <form id="editAgencyForm" class="space-y-3 text-xs" novalidate>
                <input type="hidden" id="editDocId">

                <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">ID Real</label>
                    <input type="text" id="editIdReal" required
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Zona</label>
                    <input type="text" id="editZona" list="zonesDatalist" required
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500 font-bold uppercase">
                    <datalist id="zonesDatalist"></datalist>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Dirección</label>
                    <input type="text" id="editDireccion"
                        class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                </div>

                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Latitud</label>
                        <input type="number" step="any" id="editLat" required
                            class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label class="block font-bold text-slate-400 mb-1 uppercase">Longitud</label>
                        <input type="number" step="any" id="editLng" required
                            class="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-brand-500">
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="editEnabled"
                            class="w-4 h-4 rounded bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-emerald-500 focus:ring-0 focus:ring-offset-0">
                        <span class="font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase">Agencia Activa</span>
                    </label>
                    <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">Desmarca para marcar como inactiva (se refleja en el
                        dashboard y reportes).</p>
                </div>

                <div class="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">
                    <button type="button" id="btnCancelAgencyEdit"
                        class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold rounded-xl">
                        Cancelar
                    </button>
                    <button type="submit" id="btnSaveAgency"
                        class="px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-500 disabled:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2">
                        <i class="fa fa-floppy-disk" id="btnSaveAgencyIcon"></i>
                        <span id="btnSaveAgencyText">Guardar Cambios</span>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- LIBRERÍAS -->
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/toastify-js"></script>

    <!-- LÓGICA PRINCIPAL -->
    <script type="module">
        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";

// Inicializar el sidebar dinámicamente al cargar la vista
initSidebar("sidebar-container");
        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

        import {
            collection,
            doc,
            getDoc,
            getDocs,
            updateDoc,
            serverTimestamp
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

        import {
            onAuthStateChanged,
            signOut
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // DOM
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const loadingOverlay = document.getElementById("loadingOverlay");
        const zoneFilter = document.getElementById("zoneFilter");
        const mapSearchInput = document.getElementById("mapSearchInput");
        const agencyListContainer = document.getElementById("agencyListContainer");
        const agencyListPanel = document.getElementById("agencyListPanel");
        const agencyCount = document.getElementById("agencyCount");
        const toggleListBtn = document.getElementById("toggleListBtn");
        const closeListBtn = document.getElementById("closeListBtn");
        const btnRecenter = document.getElementById("btnRecenter");

        // Modal
        const editAgencyModal = document.getElementById("editAgencyModal");
        const editAgencyForm = document.getElementById("editAgencyForm");
        const editDocId = document.getElementById("editDocId");
        const editIdReal = document.getElementById("editIdReal");
        const editZona = document.getElementById("editZona");
        const editDireccion = document.getElementById("editDireccion");
        const editLat = document.getElementById("editLat");
        const editLng = document.getElementById("editLng");
        const editEnabled = document.getElementById("editEnabled");
        const zonesDatalist = document.getElementById("zonesDatalist");
        const btnSaveAgency = document.getElementById("btnSaveAgency");
        const btnSaveAgencyIcon = document.getElementById("btnSaveAgencyIcon");
        const btnSaveAgencyText = document.getElementById("btnSaveAgencyText");


        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ESTADO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        let map = null;
        let markersGroup = null;
        let allAgencies = [];
        let zoneColorMap = {};
        let isAdmin = false;
        let mapInitialized = false;

        const ZONE_COLORS = [
            '#6366f1', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#ec4899', '#8b5cf6',
            '#06b6d4', '#f97316', '#14b8a6', '#e11d48', '#84cc16'
        ];

        function abrirNavegador(lat, lng) {
            Swal.fire({
                title: '¿Cómo deseas navegar?',
                text: 'Selecciona tu aplicación de mapas preferida',
                icon: 'question',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: '<i class="fa fa-map mr-1"></i> Google Maps',
                cancelButtonText: '<i class="fa fa-car mr-1"></i> Waze',
                confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb', // Azul Google
                cancelButtonColor: '#00d7ff',  // Celeste Waze
                background: '#0f172a',
                color: '#f8fafc',
                reverseButtons: true
            }).then((result) => {
                if (result.isConfirmed) {
                    // Opción Google Maps
                    const urlGoogle = `https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=${lat},${lng}&travelmode=driving`;
                    window.open(urlGoogle, '_blank');
                } else if (result.dismiss === Swal.DismissReason.cancel) {
                    // Opción Waze
                    const urlWaze = `https://waze.com/ul?ll=${lat},${lng}&navigate=yes`;
                    window.open(urlWaze, '_blank');
                }
            });
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // PANEL DE AGENCIAS EN MÓVIL
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        toggleListBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
            agencyListPanel.classList.add("open");
        });
        closeListBtn?.addEventListener("click", () => {
            agencyListPanel.classList.remove("open");
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // AUTENTICACIÓN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
    if (!user) {
        window.location.replace("login.html");
        return;
    }

    try {
        const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));
        if (!userSnap.exists()) { /* ...igual... */ }
        const userData = userSnap.data();
        if (userData.enabled === false) { /* ...igual... */ }

        isAdmin = userData.role === "admin";
        // 👇 El sidebar ahora se encarga de mostrar email y rol.

        if (!isAdmin) {
            document.querySelectorAll("[data-admin-only]").forEach(el => el.remove());
        }

        if (!mapInitialized) {
            initMap();
            mapInitialized = true;
        }

        await loadData();
        loadingOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
    } catch (err) { /* ...igual... */ }
});
        

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // INICIALIZACIÓN DEL MAPA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function initMap() {
            map = L.map('map', {
                zoomControl: true,
                attributionControl: false
            }).setView([18.7357, -70.1627], 8);

            L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
                maxZoom: 19
            }).addTo(map);

            L.control.attribution({ position: 'bottomleft', prefix: false })
                .addAttribution('&copy; OpenStreetMap')
                .addTo(map);

            markersGroup = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);

            btnRecenter.addEventListener("click", () => {
                const valid = allAgencies.filter(a => isValidCoord(a.lat, a.lng));
                if (valid.length === 0) return;
                const bounds = valid.map(a => [Number(a.lat), Number(a.lng)]);
                map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [50, 50] });
            });
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CARGA DE DATOS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        async function loadData() {
            try {
                const [zonesSnap, agenciasSnap] = await Promise.all([
                    getDocs(collection(db, "zonas")),
                    getDocs(collection(db, "agencias"))
                ]);

                const zonesSet = new Set();

                zonesSnap.forEach(d => {
                    const z = String(d.id || "").toUpperCase().trim();
                    if (z) zonesSet.add(z);
                });

                allAgencies = [];
                agenciasSnap.forEach(docSnap => {
                    const data = docSnap.data();

                    // Normalizar todo
                    const agencyZone = data.zona
                        ? String(data.zona).toUpperCase().trim()
                        : 'SIN ZONA';
                    if (agencyZone) zonesSet.add(agencyZone);

                    const realId = data.idReal ?? data.idreal ?? data.codigo ?? null;

                    // Leer lat/lng aceptando ambos nombres por backward compat
                    const lat = data.lat ?? data.latitud ?? null;
                    const lng = data.lng ?? data.longitud ?? null;

                    allAgencies.push({
                        docId: docSnap.id,
                        idreal: realId !== null ? String(realId) : null,
                        nombre: data.nombre || (realId ? String(realId) : 'Agencia sin nombre'),
                        zona: agencyZone,
                        lat: lat !== null ? Number(lat) : null,
                        lng: lng !== null ? Number(lng) : null,
                        enabled: data.enabled !== false,
                        direccion: data.direccion || ''
                    });
                });

                // Ordenar y poblar dropdowns
                const sortedZones = Array.from(zonesSet).sort();
                zoneFilter.innerHTML = `<option value="ALL">Todas las Zonas (${sortedZones.length})</option>`;
                zonesDatalist.innerHTML = "";
                zoneColorMap = {};

                sortedZones.forEach((zName, index) => {
                    zoneColorMap[zName] = ZONE_COLORS[index % ZONE_COLORS.length];

                    const opt = document.createElement("option");
                    opt.value = zName;
                    opt.textContent = zName;
                    zoneFilter.appendChild(opt);

                    const dlOpt = document.createElement("option");
                    dlOpt.value = zName;
                    zonesDatalist.appendChild(dlOpt);
                });

                applyFilters();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al cargar datos:", err);
                if (err.code === "permission-denied") {
                    showToast("Sin permisos para leer datos. Revisa las reglas de Firestore.", "error");
                } else {
                    showToast("Error al sincronizar con Firestore", "error");
                }
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // RENDER EN MAPA Y LISTA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function renderAgenciesOnMap(agencies) {
            markersGroup.clearLayers();
            agencyListContainer.innerHTML = "";

            const validAgencies = agencies.filter(a => isValidCoord(a.lat, a.lng));
            agencyCount.innerText = validAgencies.length;

            if (validAgencies.length === 0) {
                agencyListContainer.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-center py-8 text-slate-500 text-xs">
                        <i class="fa fa-map-pin-slash text-2xl mb-2 block text-slate-700"></i>
                        No hay agencias con coordenadas válidas.
                    </div>`;
                return;
            }

            const bounds = [];

            validAgencies.forEach(agency => {
                const lat = Number(agency.lat);
                const lng = Number(agency.lng);
                bounds.push([lat, lng]);

                const color = zoneColorMap[agency.zona] || '#6366f1';

                // ── Marcador ──
                const marker = L.circleMarker([lat, lng], {
                    radius: 8,
                    fillColor: color,
                    color: '#ffffff',
                    weight: 2,
                    opacity: 1,
                    fillOpacity: 0.95
                });

                // ── Popup construido con DOM (safe por defecto) ──
                const popup = document.createElement("div");
                popup.className = "space-y-2 text-xs min-w-[200px]";

                const header = document.createElement("div");

                if (agency.idreal) {
                    const idSpan = document.createElement("span");
                    idSpan.className = "inline-block text-[10px] font-mono font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-800 text-brand-400 uppercase";
                    idSpan.textContent = `ID: ${agency.idreal}`;
                    header.appendChild(idSpan);
                }

                const title = document.createElement("strong");
                title.className = "block text-sm font-bold text-white mt-1";
                title.textContent = agency.nombre;
                header.appendChild(title);

                const addr = document.createElement("p");
                addr.className = "text-slate-400 mt-1";
                addr.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-location-dot text-xs mr-1" style="color:${color}"></i>`;
                addr.appendChild(document.createTextNode(agency.direccion || 'Sin dirección'));
                header.appendChild(addr);

                popup.appendChild(header);

                const badges = document.createElement("div");
                badges.className = "flex items-center gap-2";

                const zoneBadge = document.createElement("span");
                zoneBadge.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold text-white uppercase";
                zoneBadge.style.backgroundColor = color;
                zoneBadge.textContent = agency.zona;
                badges.appendChild(zoneBadge);

                const statusBadge = document.createElement("span");
                statusBadge.className = `px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase ${agency.enabled
                        ? "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400"
                        : "bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400"
                    }`;
                statusBadge.textContent = agency.enabled ? "Activa" : "Inactiva";
                badges.appendChild(statusBadge);

                popup.appendChild(badges);

                // Botón de navegación Waze / Maps
const btnNavegar = document.createElement("button");
btnNavegar.className = "w-full mt-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer transition-colors";
btnNavegar.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-location-arrow"></i> Navegar a Agencia`;
btnNavegar.addEventListener("click", () => {
    abrirNavegador(lat, lng);
});
popup.appendChild(btnNavegar);

                // Botón de editar (solo admin)
                if (isAdmin) {
                    const btnEdit = document.createElement("button");
                    btnEdit.className = "w-full mt-2 bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-500 text-white font-bold py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer transition-colors";
                    btnEdit.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-pen-to-square"></i> Editar Agencia`;
                    btnEdit.addEventListener("click", () => {
                        map.closePopup();
                        openEditModal(agency);
                    });
                    popup.appendChild(btnEdit);
                }

                marker.bindPopup(popup);
                markersGroup.addLayer(marker);

                // ── Tarjeta lateral ──
                const card = document.createElement("div");
                card.className = "p-3 bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800 hover:border-brand-500/50 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-all space-y-1";

                const cardHeader = document.createElement("div");
                cardHeader.className = "flex justify-between items-center gap-2";

                const cardTitle = document.createElement("h4");
                cardTitle.className = "text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 truncate";
                cardTitle.textContent = agency.nombre;
                cardHeader.appendChild(cardTitle);

                const dot = document.createElement("span");
                dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0";
                dot.style.backgroundColor = color;
                cardHeader.appendChild(dot);

                card.appendChild(cardHeader);

                if (agency.idreal) {
                    const idLine = document.createElement("p");
                    idLine.className = "text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500";
                    idLine.textContent = `ID: ${agency.idreal}`;
                    card.appendChild(idLine);
                }

                const zoneLine = document.createElement("p");
                zoneLine.className = "text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider";
                zoneLine.style.color = color;
                zoneLine.textContent = agency.zona;
                card.appendChild(zoneLine);

                const addrLine = document.createElement("p");
                addrLine.className = "text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate";
                addrLine.textContent = agency.direccion || 'Sin dirección';
                card.appendChild(addrLine);

                card.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    map.flyTo([lat, lng], 16, { duration: 1.2 });
                    // Cerrar panel en mobile
                    agencyListPanel.classList.remove("open");
                });

                agencyListContainer.appendChild(card);
            });

            // Ajustar vista
            if (bounds.length > 0) {
                map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [50, 50] });
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // MODAL EDITAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function openEditModal(agency) {
            editDocId.value = agency.docId;
            editIdReal.value = agency.idreal || "";
            editZona.value = agency.zona || "";
            editDireccion.value = agency.direccion || "";
            editLat.value = agency.lat ?? "";
            editLng.value = agency.lng ?? "";
            editEnabled.checked = agency.enabled;

            editAgencyModal.classList.remove("hidden");
            setTimeout(() => editIdReal.focus(), 50);
        }

        function closeEditModal() {
            editAgencyModal.classList.add("hidden");
            editAgencyForm.reset();
            editDocId.value = "";
        }

        document.getElementById("btnCloseModal").addEventListener("click", closeEditModal);
        document.getElementById("btnCancelAgencyEdit").addEventListener("click", closeEditModal);
        editAgencyModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            if (e.target === editAgencyModal) closeEditModal();
        });
        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape" && !editAgencyModal.classList.contains("hidden")) closeEditModal();
        });

        editAgencyForm.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (!isAdmin) {
                showToast("Solo administradores pueden editar agencias.", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (btnSaveAgency.disabled) return;

            const docId = editDocId.value;
            if (!docId) return;

            const newIdReal = editIdReal.value.trim();
            const newZona = editZona.value.trim().toUpperCase();
            const newDireccion = editDireccion.value.trim();
            const newLat = parseFloat(editLat.value);
            const newLng = parseFloat(editLng.value);
            const newEnabled = editEnabled.checked;

            // Validaciones
            if (!newIdReal || !newZona) {
                showToast("ID Real y Zona son obligatorios.", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (isNaN(newLat) || isNaN(newLng)) {
                showToast("Latitud y Longitud deben ser números válidos.", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (newLat < -90 || newLat > 90 || newLng < -180 || newLng > 180) {
                showToast("Coordenadas fuera de rango válido.", "error");
                return;
            }

            btnSaveAgency.disabled = true;
            btnSaveAgencyText.textContent = "Guardando...";
            btnSaveAgencyIcon.className = "fa fa-circle-notch fa-spin";

            try {
                await updateDoc(doc(db, "agencias", docId), {
                    idReal: newIdReal,
                    zona: newZona,
                    direccion: newDireccion,
                    lat: newLat,
                    lng: newLng,
                    enabled: newEnabled,
                    updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
                    // NOTA: ya no escribimos `nombre` ni `estado` para mantener consistencia
                });

                // Actualizar array local (sin recargar todo)
                const idx = allAgencies.findIndex(a => a.docId === docId);
                if (idx !== -1) {
                    allAgencies[idx] = {
                        ...allAgencies[idx],
                        idreal: newIdReal,
                        nombre: allAgencies[idx].nombre || newIdReal,
                        zona: newZona,
                        direccion: newDireccion,
                        lat: newLat,
                        lng: newLng,
                        enabled: newEnabled
                    };
                }

                showToast("Agencia actualizada correctamente.", "success");
                closeEditModal();
                applyFilters();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al guardar:", err);
                if (err.code === "permission-denied") {
                    showToast("Sin permisos. Las reglas de Firestore rechazaron la operación.", "error");
                } else {
                    showToast("Error al guardar los cambios.", "error");
                }
            } finally {
                btnSaveAgency.disabled = false;
                btnSaveAgencyText.textContent = "Guardar Cambios";
                btnSaveAgencyIcon.className = "fa fa-floppy-disk";
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // FILTROS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function applyFilters() {
            const selectedZone = zoneFilter.value;
            const searchText = mapSearchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();

            const filtered = allAgencies.filter(agency => {
                const matchesZone = selectedZone === "ALL" || agency.zona === selectedZone;

                const idr = String(agency.idreal ?? "").toLowerCase();
                const name = String(agency.nombre ?? "").toLowerCase();
                const addr = String(agency.direccion ?? "").toLowerCase();

                const matchesSearch = !searchText ||
                    idr.includes(searchText) ||
                    name.includes(searchText) ||
                    addr.includes(searchText);

                return matchesZone && matchesSearch;
            });

            renderAgenciesOnMap(filtered);
        }

        // Debounce en búsqueda
        let searchTimer = null;
        mapSearchInput.addEventListener("input", () => {
            clearTimeout(searchTimer);
            searchTimer = setTimeout(applyFilters, 220);
        });
        zoneFilter.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // UTILIDADES
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function isValidCoord(lat, lng) {
            if (lat === null || lng === null || lat === undefined || lng === undefined) return false;
            const la = Number(lat), lo = Number(lng);
            return !isNaN(la) && !isNaN(lo) && la !== 0 && lo !== 0;
        }

        function showToast(msg, type = "info") {
            const bg = type === "success"
                ? "linear-gradient(to right, #059669, #10b981)"
                : type === "error"
                    ? "linear-gradient(to right, #e11d48, #f43f5e)"
                    : "linear-gradient(to right, #4f46e5, #6366f1)";
            Toastify({
                text: msg,
                duration: 3200,
                gravity: "bottom",
                position: "right",
                style: { background: bg, borderRadius: "10px", fontSize: "12px", fontWeight: "600" }
            }).showToast();
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>

<link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="./img/favicon.svg">

    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <link href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.1/css/all.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11/dist/sweetalert2.min.css" rel="stylesheet">

                <div class="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-5 rounded-3xl shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-between print-break">
                    <div class="min-w-0">
                        <p class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Zona Más Cargada</p>
                        <h3 id="kpiTopZone" class="text-base font-bold text-white mt-1 truncate">—</h3>
                        <p id="kpiTopZoneSub" class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">&nbsp;</p>
