    <!-- Lógica principal -->
    <script type="module">

        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";                    // 👈 AGREGAR
        initSidebar("sidebar-container");

        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";
        import { collection, getDocs, doc, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";
        import { onAuthStateChanged, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        // DOM
        const bootOverlay = document.getElementById("bootOverlay");
        const mainContent = document.getElementById("mainContent");
        const filterZona = document.getElementById("filterZona");
        const filterEstado = document.getElementById("filterEstado");
        const searchQuery = document.getElementById("searchQuery");
        const btnResetFilters = document.getElementById("btnResetFilters");
        const reportTableBody = document.getElementById("reportTableBody");
        const btnExportExcel = document.getElementById("btnExportExcel");
        const btnPrintReport = document.getElementById("btnPrintReport");
        const btnReload = document.getElementById("btnReload");
        const lastUpdate = document.getElementById("lastUpdate");
        const filteredCountBadge = document.getElementById("filteredCountBadge");
        const tableLimitBadge = document.getElementById("tableLimitBadge");
        const loadMoreContainer = document.getElementById("loadMoreContainer");
        const btnLoadMore = document.getElementById("btnLoadMore");
        const btnLoadMoreText = document.getElementById("btnLoadMoreText");
        const printDate = document.getElementById("printDate");

        const userEmailSpan = document.getElementById("userEmail");
        const userRoleSpan = document.getElementById("userRole");
        const btnLogout = document.getElementById("btnLogout");

        let rawAgencies = [];
        let filteredAgencies = [];
        let zoneChartInstance = null;
        let completenessChartInstance = null;
        let visibleRowCount = 100;
        const ROW_CHUNK = 100;

        const CHART_COLORS = [
            '#6366f1', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#ec4899', '#8b5cf6',
            '#06b6d4', '#f97316', '#14b8a6', '#e11d48', '#84cc16'
        ];

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }

            userEmailSpan.innerText = user.email;

            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));
                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    await Swal.fire("Cuenta no registrada", "Contacta al administrador.", "error");
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const userData = userSnap.data();
                if (userData.enabled === false) {
                    await Swal.fire("Usuario inhabilitado", "Tu acceso ha sido revocado.", "warning");
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const isAdmin = userData.role === "admin";
                userRoleSpan.innerText = isAdmin ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador";
                userRoleSpan.className = isAdmin
                    ? "text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wider"
                    : "text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider";

                if (!isAdmin) {
                    document.querySelectorAll("[data-admin-only]").forEach(el => el.remove());
                }

                bootOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
                mainContent.classList.remove("hidden");

                await loadReportData();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al validar sesión:", err);
                Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudieron verificar tus credenciales.", "error");
                window.location.replace("login.html");
            }
        });

        
btnLogout.addEventListener("click", async () => {
    const result = await Swal.fire({
        title: '¿Cerrar sesión?',
        text: '¿Estás seguro de que deseas salir de tu cuenta?',
        icon: 'question',
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#dc2626',
        cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
        confirmButtonText: 'Sí, cerrar sesión',
        cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar',
        reverseButtons: true,
        focusCancel: true
    });

    if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

    try {
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Cerrando sesión...',
            text: 'Por favor, espera.',
            allowOutsideClick: false,
            allowEscapeKey: false,
            didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading()
        });

        await signOut(auth);

        window.location.replace("login.html");

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error al cerrar sesión:', error);

        Swal.fire({
            icon: 'error',
            title: '¡Error!',
            text: 'No se pudo cerrar la sesión. Inténtalo nuevamente.',
            confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
            confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
        });
    }
});


        async function loadReportData() {
            try {
                reportTableBody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-8 text-slate-500">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin mr-2"></i>Cargando datos...
                    </td></tr>`;

                const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, "agencias"));
                rawAgencies = [];

                snap.forEach(docSnap => {
                    const data = docSnap.data();
                    const realId = data.idReal ?? data.idreal ?? data.codigo ?? null;
                    const lat = data.lat ?? data.latitud ?? null;
                    const lng = data.lng ?? data.longitud ?? null;
                    const enabled = data.enabled !== false &&
                        String(data.estado || "Activa").toLowerCase() !== "inactiva";
                    const zona = data.zona ? String(data.zona).toUpperCase().trim() : "SIN ZONA";

                    rawAgencies.push({
                        docId: docSnap.id,
                        idReal: realId !== null ? String(realId) : "",
                        zona,
                        direccion: String(data.direccion || "").trim(),
                        lat: lat !== null ? Number(lat) : 0,
                        lng: lng !== null ? Number(lng) : 0,
                        enabled
                    });
                });

                rawAgencies.sort((a, b) =>
                    a.idReal.localeCompare(b.idReal, undefined, { numeric: true })
                );

                populateZoneFilter();

                const now = new Date();
                lastUpdate.textContent = `· Actualizado ${now.toLocaleTimeString("es-DO", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}`;
                printDate.textContent = now.toLocaleString("es-DO");

                applyFilters();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al cargar datos:", err);
                let msg = "No se pudieron obtener los datos de Firestore.";
                reportTableBody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-8 text-rose-400 font-medium">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation mr-2"></i>${msg}
                    </td></tr>`;
                Swal.fire("Error", msg, "error");
            }
        }

        function populateZoneFilter() {
            const zones = new Set();
            rawAgencies.forEach(a => { if (a.zona) zones.add(a.zona); });

            const current = filterZona.value;
            filterZona.innerHTML = `<option value="ALL">Todas las Zonas (${zones.size})</option>`;
            Array.from(zones).sort().forEach(z => {
                const opt = document.createElement("option");
                opt.value = z;
                opt.textContent = z;
                filterZona.appendChild(opt);
            });

            if (current && (current === "ALL" || zones.has(current))) {
                filterZona.value = current;
            }
        }

        function applyFilters() {
            const selectedZone = filterZona.value;
            const selectedEstado = filterEstado.value;
            const queryText = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim();

            filteredAgencies = rawAgencies.filter(agency => {
                if (selectedZone !== "ALL" && agency.zona !== selectedZone) return false;
                if (selectedEstado === "ENABLED" && !agency.enabled) return false;
                if (selectedEstado === "DISABLED" && agency.enabled) return false;

                if (queryText) {
                    const inId = agency.idReal.toLowerCase().includes(queryText);
                    const inDir = agency.direccion.toLowerCase().includes(queryText);
                    const inZone = agency.zona.toLowerCase().includes(queryText);
                    if (!inId && !inDir && !inZone) return false;
                }
                return true;
            });

            visibleRowCount = ROW_CHUNK;
            updateKPIs();
            renderCharts();
            renderTable();
        }

        let searchTimer = null;
        searchQuery.addEventListener("input", () => {
            clearTimeout(searchTimer);
            searchTimer = setTimeout(applyFilters, 220);
        });
        filterZona.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);
        filterEstado.addEventListener("change", applyFilters);

        btnResetFilters.addEventListener("click", () => {
            filterZona.value = "ALL";
            filterEstado.value = "ALL";
            searchQuery.value = "";
            applyFilters();
        });

        function updateKPIs() {
            const totalGlobal = rawAgencies.length;
            const totalFiltered = filteredAgencies.length;

            document.getElementById("kpiTotalAgencies").textContent = totalFiltered.toLocaleString();
            document.getElementById("kpiTotalAgenciesSub").textContent =
                totalFiltered === totalGlobal
                    ? `Total del sistema: ${totalGlobal.toLocaleString()}`
                    : `de ${totalGlobal.toLocaleString()} totales`;

            const zonesMap = {};
            filteredAgencies.forEach(a => { zonesMap[a.zona] = (zonesMap[a.zona] || 0) + 1; });
            const uniqueZones = Object.keys(zonesMap).length;
            document.getElementById("kpiTotalZones").textContent = uniqueZones;

            const topZoneEntry = Object.entries(zonesMap).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
            document.getElementById("kpiTotalZonesSub").textContent = topZoneEntry
                ? `Líder: ${topZoneEntry[0]}`
                : "\u00A0";

            const geolocated = filteredAgencies.filter(a => a.lat && a.lng && a.lat !== 0 && a.lng !== 0).length;
            const geoPerc = totalFiltered > 0 ? Math.round((geolocated / totalFiltered) * 100) : 0;
            document.getElementById("kpiGeolocated").textContent = `${geoPerc}%`;
            document.getElementById("kpiGeolocatedSub").textContent = `${geolocated} de ${totalFiltered}`;

            if (topZoneEntry) {
                document.getElementById("kpiTopZone").textContent = topZoneEntry[0];
                document.getElementById("kpiTopZoneSub").textContent = `${topZoneEntry[1]} ${topZoneEntry[1] === 1 ? "agencia" : "agencias"}`;
            } else {
                document.getElementById("kpiTopZone").textContent = "—";
                document.getElementById("kpiTopZoneSub").textContent = "\u00A0";
            }

            filteredCountBadge.textContent = `${totalFiltered.toLocaleString()} registro${totalFiltered === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
        }

        function renderCharts() {
            const zonesMap = {};
            let geolocated = 0, missingGeo = 0;

            filteredAgencies.forEach(a => {
                const z = a.zona || "Sin Zona";
                zonesMap[z] = (zonesMap[z] || 0) + 1;
                if (a.lat && a.lng && a.lat !== 0 && a.lng !== 0) geolocated++;
                else missingGeo++;
            });

            const sortedZones = Object.entries(zonesMap).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
            const labels = sortedZones.map(([z]) => z);
            const values = sortedZones.map(([, v]) => v);
            const bgColors = labels.map((_, i) => CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length]);

            if (zoneChartInstance) {
                zoneChartInstance.data.labels = labels;
                zoneChartInstance.data.datasets[0].data = values;
                zoneChartInstance.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = bgColors;
                zoneChartInstance.update();
            } else {
                const ctx = document.getElementById("zoneChart").getContext("2d");
                zoneChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
                    type: "bar",
                    data: {
                        labels,
                        datasets: [{ label: "Agencias", data: values, backgroundColor: bgColors, borderRadius: 8 }]
                    },
                    options: {
                        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
                        plugins: {
                            legend: { display: false },
                            tooltip: { callbacks: { label: (ctx) => `${ctx.parsed.y} agencias` } }
                        },
                        scales: {
                            y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { precision: 0, color: '#94a3b8' }, grid: { color: 'rgba(51, 65, 85, 0.2)' } },
                            x: { ticks: { color: '#94a3b8' }, grid: { display: false } }
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

            if (completenessChartInstance) {
                completenessChartInstance.data.datasets[0].data = [geolocated, missingGeo];
                completenessChartInstance.update();
            } else {
                const ctx = document.getElementById("completenessChart").getContext("2d");
                completenessChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
                    type: "doughnut",
                    data: {
                        labels: ["Con Coordenadas", "Sin Coordenadas"],
                        datasets: [{ data: [geolocated, missingGeo], backgroundColor: ["#10b981", "#f59e0b"], borderWidth: 0 }]
                    },
                    options: {
                        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, cutout: "62%",
                        plugins: {
                            legend: { position: "bottom", labels: { color: '#cbd5e1', font: { family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans' } } },
                            tooltip: {
                                callbacks: {
                                    label: (ctx) => {
                                        const total = ctx.dataset.data.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
                                        const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((ctx.parsed / total) * 100) : 0;
                                        return `${ctx.label}: ${ctx.parsed} (${pct}%)`;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        function renderTable() {
            reportTableBody.innerHTML = "";

            if (filteredAgencies.length === 0) {
                reportTableBody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-10 text-slate-500">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-inbox text-3xl text-slate-600 mb-2 block"></i>
                        No se encontraron agencias con los filtros seleccionados.
                    </td></tr>`;
                loadMoreContainer.classList.add("hidden");
                tableLimitBadge.textContent = "";
                return;
            }

            const visible = filteredAgencies.slice(0, visibleRowCount);

            visible.forEach((item, index) => {
                const tr = document.createElement("tr");
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition-colors";

                tr.appendChild(cellText(String(index + 1), "py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-500"));

                const tdId = document.createElement("td");
                tdId.className = "py-3 px-4";
                const spanId = document.createElement("span");
                spanId.className = "px-2 py-0.5 bg-brand-500/10 text-brand-400 font-mono font-bold rounded border border-brand-500/20";
                spanId.textContent = item.idReal || "—";
                tdId.appendChild(spanId);
                tr.appendChild(tdId);

                const tdZona = document.createElement("td");
                tdZona.className = "py-3 px-4";
                const spanZona = document.createElement("span");
                spanZona.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 font-medium text-[10px]";
                spanZona.textContent = item.zona || "—";
                tdZona.appendChild(spanZona);
                tr.appendChild(tdZona);

                tr.appendChild(cellText(item.direccion || "—", "py-3 px-4 font-medium text-slate-200 max-w-xs truncate"));

                const tdCoords = document.createElement("td");
                tdCoords.className = "py-3 px-4";
                const hasCoords = item.lat && item.lng && item.lat !== 0 && item.lng !== 0;
                if (hasCoords) {
                    const spanC = document.createElement("span");
                    spanC.className = "px-2 py-0.5 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 font-mono text-[11px] rounded border border-emerald-500/20";
                    spanC.textContent = `${item.lat.toFixed(4)}, ${item.lng.toFixed(4)}`;
                    tdCoords.appendChild(spanC);
                } else {
                    const spanC = document.createElement("span");
                    spanC.className = "px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 text-[11px] rounded border border-amber-500/20";
                    spanC.textContent = "Pendiente";
                    tdCoords.appendChild(spanC);
                }
                tr.appendChild(tdCoords);

                const tdEstado = document.createElement("td");
                tdEstado.className = "py-3 px-4 text-center no-print";
                const badge = document.createElement("span");
                badge.className = item.enabled
                    ? "inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20"
                    : "inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/20";
                badge.innerHTML = item.enabled
                    ? '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400"></span>Activa'
                    : '<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-400"></span>Inactiva';
                tdEstado.appendChild(badge);
                tr.appendChild(tdEstado);

                reportTableBody.appendChild(tr);
            });

            const remaining = filteredAgencies.length - visible.length;
            if (remaining > 0) {
                loadMoreContainer.classList.remove("hidden");
                btnLoadMoreText.textContent = `Mostrar ${Math.min(ROW_CHUNK, remaining)} más (${remaining} restantes)`;
                tableLimitBadge.textContent = `Mostrando ${visible.length.toLocaleString()} de ${filteredAgencies.length.toLocaleString()}`;
            } else {
                loadMoreContainer.classList.add("hidden");
                tableLimitBadge.textContent = `Mostrando todos (${filteredAgencies.length.toLocaleString()})`;
            }
        }

        function cellText(text, className) {
            const td = document.createElement("td");
            td.className = className;
            td.textContent = text;
            return td;
        }

        btnLoadMore.addEventListener("click", () => {
            visibleRowCount += ROW_CHUNK;
            renderTable();
        });

        btnExportExcel.addEventListener("click", () => {
            if (filteredAgencies.length === 0) {
                Swal.fire("Atención", "No hay datos para exportar.", "warning");
                return;
            }

            const exportData = filteredAgencies.map((a, index) => ({
                "#": index + 1,
                "ID Real": a.idReal || "",
                "Zona": a.zona || "",
                "Dirección": a.direccion || "",
                "Latitud": a.lat || 0,
                "Longitud": a.lng || 0,
                "Estado": a.enabled ? "Activa" : "Inactiva"
            }));

            const worksheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(exportData);
            const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new();
            XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, "Reporte_Agencias");
            XLSX.writeFile(workbook, `Reporte_Agencias_${dateStamp()}.xlsx`);
        });

        btnPrintReport.addEventListener("click", () => { window.print(); });

        btnReload.addEventListener("click", async () => {
            btnReload.disabled = true;
            btnReload.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin text-xs"></i> Cargando...`;
            await loadReportData();
            btnReload.disabled = false;
            btnReload.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-right text-xs"></i> Recargar`;
        });

        function dateStamp() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                <span>Partida</span>
            </button>

    <script type="module">
        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";                   // 👈 AGREGAR

        setTimeout(() => {
            const ov = document.getElementById("bootOverlay");
            if (ov && ov.style.display !== "none") {
                ov.style.display = "none";
            }
        }, 8000);

        const OSRM_BASE = "https://router.project-osrm.org";
        const MAX_STOPS = 90;
        const DEFAULT_CENTER = [18.7357, -70.1627];

        let map = null;
        let markersLayer = null;
        let routeLayer = null;
        let userLiveMarker = null;
        let travelHistory = [];
        let travelTrailLayer = null;

        let watchId = null; // ID del rastreo en tiempo real


        btnLogout.addEventListener("click", async () => {
            const res = await Swal.fire({
                title: "¿Cerrar sesión?",
                icon: "question",
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: "Sí, salir",
                cancelButtonText: "Cancelar",
                confirmButtonColor: "#e11d48",
                cancelButtonColor: "#475569"
            });
            if (!res.isConfirmed) return;
            if (watchId !== null) navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchId);
            await signOut(auth);
            window.location.replace("login.html");
        });

        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }

            userEmailSpan.innerText = user.email;

            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));
                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    await Swal.fire("Cuenta no registrada", "Contacta al administrador.", "error");
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const userData = userSnap.data();
                if (userData.enabled === false) {
                    await Swal.fire("Usuario inhabilitado", "Tu acceso ha sido revocado.", "warning");
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                isAdmin = userData.role === "admin";
                userRoleSpan.innerText = isAdmin ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador";

                if (!isAdmin) {
                    document.querySelectorAll("[data-admin-only]").forEach(el => el.remove());
                }

                if (!mapInitialized) {
                    initMap();
                    mapInitialized = true;
                }

                setupBottomSheet();
                setupSheetTabs();
                setupProfileButtons();
                setupStartModeButtons();
                setupEventListeners();

                await loadAgencies();
                bootOverlay.style.display = "none";

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("[boot] ❌ ERROR:", err);
                window.location.replace("login.html");
            }
        });

        function initMap() {
            map = L.map("map", {
                zoomControl: true,
                attributionControl: true
            }).setView(DEFAULT_CENTER, 8);

            L.tileLayer("https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png", {
                maxZoom: 19,
                attribution: "&copy; OSM"
            }).addTo(map);

            markersLayer = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
            routeLayer = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
            travelTrailLayer = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);

            btnRecenter.addEventListener("click", fitAllAgencies);
        }

        function fitAllAgencies() {
            if (allAgencies.length === 0) return;
            const bounds = allAgencies.map(a => [a.lat, a.lng]);
            map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 13 });
        }

        function setupBottomSheet() {
            let startY = 0;
            let startTransform = 0;
            let currentTransform = 0;
            let isDragging = false;

            const getSheetHeight = () => bottomSheet.offsetHeight;

            function getTransformForState(state) {
                const h = getSheetHeight();
                if (state === "peek") return h - 120;
                if (state === "half") return h - window.innerHeight * 0.5;
                if (state === "expanded") return 0;
                return h - 120;
            }

            function setState(state, animate = true) {
                currentSheetState = state;
                document.body.dataset.sheet = state;
                if (!animate) bottomSheet.style.transition = "none";
                bottomSheet.style.transform = `translateY(${getTransformForState(state)}px)`;
                if (!animate) {
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => { bottomSheet.style.transition = ""; });
                }
            }

            setState("peek");

            const getY = (e) => e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;

            function dragStart(e) {
                if (window.innerWidth >= 768) return;
                isDragging = true;
                startY = getY(e);
                const rect = bottomSheet.getBoundingClientRect();
                startTransform = rect.top;
                bottomSheet.style.transition = "none";
                document.body.style.userSelect = "none";
            }

            function dragMove(e) {
                if (!isDragging) return;
                e.preventDefault();
                const deltaY = getY(e) - startY;
                const newTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 60, startTransform + deltaY));
                bottomSheet.style.transform = `translateY(${newTop}px)`;
                currentTransform = newTop;
            }

            function dragEnd() {
                if (!isDragging) return;
                isDragging = false;
                document.body.style.userSelect = "";
                bottomSheet.style.transition = "";

                const h = getSheetHeight();
                const peekY = h - 120;
                const halfY = h - window.innerHeight * 0.5;

                const distances = [
                    { state: "peek",     dist: Math.abs(currentTransform - peekY) },
                    { state: "half",     dist: Math.abs(currentTransform - halfY) },
                    { state: "expanded", dist: Math.abs(currentTransform - 0) }
                ];
                distances.sort((a, b) => a.dist - b.dist);
                setState(distances[0].state);
            }

            dragHandle.addEventListener("touchstart", dragStart, { passive: true });
            dragHandle.addEventListener("touchmove", dragMove, { passive: false });
            dragHandle.addEventListener("touchend", dragEnd);

            dragHandle.addEventListener("mousedown", dragStart);
            document.addEventListener("mousemove", dragMove);
            document.addEventListener("mouseup", dragEnd);

            window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
                if (window.innerWidth >= 768) return;
                setState(currentSheetState, false);
            });
        }

        function setupSheetTabs() {
            const tabs = document.querySelectorAll(".sheet-tab");
            tabs.forEach(tab => {
                tab.addEventListener("click", () => switchSheetTab(tab.dataset.tab));
            });
        }

        function switchSheetTab(target) {
            document.querySelectorAll(".sheet-tab").forEach(t => {
                t.classList.toggle("active", t.dataset.tab === target);
            });
            document.querySelectorAll(".sheet-panel").forEach(p => {
                p.classList.toggle("active", p.dataset.panel === target);
            });

            if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
                const sheetHeight = bottomSheet.offsetHeight;
                bottomSheet.style.transform = `translateY(${sheetHeight - window.innerHeight * 0.5}px)`;
                document.body.dataset.sheet = "half";
                currentSheetState = "half";
            }
        }

        function setupProfileButtons() {
            const btns = document.querySelectorAll(".profile-btn");
            btns.forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    btns.forEach(b => {
                        b.className = "profile-btn py-3 rounded-xl border transition-all flex flex-col items-center gap-1 border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-400";
                    });
                    btn.className = "profile-btn py-3 rounded-xl border transition-all flex flex-col items-center gap-1 border-brand-500 bg-brand-600/20 text-brand-300";
                    profileInput.value = btn.dataset.profile;
                });
            });
            btns[0].click();
        }

        function setupStartModeButtons() {
            const btns = document.querySelectorAll(".start-mode-btn");
            btns.forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                    const mode = btn.dataset.startMode;
                    btns.forEach(b => {
                        b.className = "start-mode-btn py-3 rounded-xl border transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold";
                    });
                    btn.className = "start-mode-btn py-3 rounded-xl border transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-brand-500 bg-brand-600/20 text-brand-300 text-xs font-bold";

                    manualStartPanel.classList.toggle("hidden", mode !== "manual");
                    agencyStartPanel.classList.toggle("hidden", mode !== "agency");
                });
            });
        }

        function setupEventListeners() {
            btnUseMyLocation.addEventListener("click", useMyLocation);
            btnSetManualStart.addEventListener("click", setManualStart);
            btnClearStart.addEventListener("click", clearStartPoint);
            btnGoToAgencies.addEventListener("click", () => switchSheetTab("agencies"));

            fabCalculate.addEventListener("click", calculateRoute);
            btnCalculateDesktop.addEventListener("click", calculateRoute);
            btnRecenter.addEventListener("click", fitAllAgencies);

            btnSelectAllFiltered.addEventListener("click", selectAllFiltered);
            btnDeselectAll.addEventListener("click", () => {
                selectedIds.clear();
                updateSelectionUI();
                renderAgencyList();
            });

            agencySearch.addEventListener("input", debounce(() => {
                btnClearSearch.classList.toggle("hidden", !agencySearch.value);
                renderAgencyList();
            }, 200));
            btnClearSearch.addEventListener("click", () => {
                agencySearch.value = "";
                btnClearSearch.classList.add("hidden");
                renderAgencyList();
            });
            zoneFilterList.addEventListener("change", renderAgencyList);

            btnOpenGoogleMaps.addEventListener("click", openInNavigationApps);
            btnShowDetails.addEventListener("click", showStopsModal);
            btnExportRouteExcel.addEventListener("click", exportRouteToExcel);
            btnSaveRoute.addEventListener("click", showSaveRouteModal);
            btnMarkNext.addEventListener("click", markNextStop);
            btnResetProgress.addEventListener("click", resetProgress);

            btnCloseStopsModal.addEventListener("click", () => stopsModal.classList.add("hidden"));
            stopsModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => { if (e.target === stopsModal) stopsModal.classList.add("hidden"); });

            btnCloseSaveModal.addEventListener("click", () => saveRouteModal.classList.add("hidden"));
            btnCancelSaveRoute.addEventListener("click", () => saveRouteModal.classList.add("hidden"));
            saveRouteModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => { if (e.target === saveRouteModal) saveRouteModal.classList.add("hidden"); });
            btnConfirmSaveRoute.addEventListener("click", saveRoute);

            document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
                if (e.key === "Escape") {
                    stopsModal.classList.add("hidden");
                    saveRouteModal.classList.add("hidden");
                    closeMenu();
                }
            });
        }

        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // RASTREO GPS EN TIEMPO REAL (CORREGIDO)
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

        function updateTravelTrail() {
    if (!travelTrailLayer) return;
    travelTrailLayer.clearLayers();

    if (travelHistory.length < 2) return;

    // Estela más ancha y llamativa por donde vas pasando
    const trailLine = L.polyline(travelHistory, {
        color: '#00ffff',     // Color cian brillante o el que prefieras (#3b82f6 para azul)
        weight: 10,           // Grosor ancho (mayor que la ruta principal que suele ser de 5)
        opacity: 0.6,         // Semi-transparente para que luzca como una estela
        lineCap: 'round',
        lineJoin: 'round'
    });

    travelTrailLayer.addLayer(trailLine);
}
        function useMyLocation() {
            if (!navigator.geolocation) {
                geoStatus.textContent = "Tu navegador no soporta geolocalización.";
                geoStatus.className = "text-xs text-rose-400 text-center";
                return;
            }

            btnUseMyLocation.disabled = true;
            btnUseMyLocationText.textContent = "Activando GPS en vivo...";
            geoStatus.textContent = "Permite el acceso cuando el navegador lo pida...";
            geoStatus.className = "text-xs text-amber-400 text-center";

            // Limpiar watch anterior si ya existía
            if (watchId !== null) {
                navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchId);
            }

            watchId = navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
                (pos) => {
                    const lat = pos.coords.latitude;
                    const lng = pos.coords.longitude;
                    const liveLabel = "Mi ubicación actual (En vivo)";


                    travelHistory.push([lat, lng]);
                    updateTravelTrail();
                    // 1. Si aún no hay un punto de partida definido, lo establecemos por primera vez
                    if (!startPoint) {
                        setStartPoint({ lat, lng, label: liveLabel }, true);
                    } else {
                        // 2. Si ya hay ruta o punto fijo, SOLO actualizamos el marcador flotante en tiempo real sin borrar nada
                        updateLiveUserMarker(lat, lng, liveLabel);
                    }

                    geoStatus.textContent = `✓ GPS Activo (Precisión: ±${Math.round(pos.coords.accuracy)}m)`;
                    geoStatus.className = "text-xs text-emerald-400 text-center";
                    btnUseMyLocation.disabled = false;
                    btnUseMyLocationText.textContent = "Ubicación en vivo activa";
                },
                (err) => {
                    let msg = "No se pudo obtener la ubicación.";
                    if (err.code === 1) msg = "Permiso denegado.";
                    if (err.code === 2) msg = "Ubicación no disponible.";
                    if (err.code === 3) msg = "Tiempo de espera agotado.";
                    geoStatus.textContent = msg;
                    geoStatus.className = "text-xs text-rose-400 text-center";
                    btnUseMyLocation.disabled = false;
                    btnUseMyLocationText.textContent = "Usar mi ubicación actual";
                },
                { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 15000, maximumAge: 5000 }
            );
        }
        
        function updateLiveUserMarker(lat, lng, label) {
            const liveIcon = L.divIcon({
                className: "",
                html: `<div class="stop-marker start animate-pulse" style="background-color: #3b82f6;"><i class="fa fa-location-arrow"></i></div>`,
                iconSize: [38, 38],
                iconAnchor: [19, 19]
            });

            if (userLiveMarker) {
                userLiveMarker.setLatLng([lat, lng]);
            } else {
                userLiveMarker = L.marker([lat, lng], { icon: liveIcon, zIndexOffset: 2000 })
                    .bindPopup(`<strong>📍 Tu posición en vivo</strong><br>${escapeHtml(label)}`)
                    .addTo(map);
            }
        }
        function setManualStart() {
            const lat = parseFloat(document.getElementById("startLat").value);
            const lng = parseFloat(document.getElementById("startLng").value);

            if (isNaN(lat) || isNaN(lng)) {
                Swal.fire("Datos inválidos", "Ingresa latitud y longitud numéricas.", "warning");
                return;
            }
            if (lat < -90 || lat > 90 || lng < -180 || lng > 180) {
                Swal.fire("Fuera de rango", "Lat entre -90/90, Lng entre -180/180.", "warning");
                return;
            }

            setStartPoint({ lat, lng, label: "Coordenadas manuales" }, true);
        }

        function setStartPoint(point, fly = true) {
            startPoint = point;
            startPointLabel.textContent = point.label;
            startPointCoords.textContent = `${point.lat.toFixed(6)}, ${point.lng.toFixed(6)}`;
            startPointStatus.classList.remove("hidden");

            renderAgencyList();
            updateSelectionUI();

            if (fly) {
                map.flyTo([point.lat, point.lng], 14, { duration: 1 });
            }

            routeLayer.clearLayers();
            const startIcon = L.divIcon({
                className: "",
                html: `<div class="stop-marker start"><i class="fa fa-flag-checkered"></i></div>`,
                iconSize: [38, 38],
                iconAnchor: [19, 19]
            });
            L.marker([point.lat, point.lng], { icon: startIcon, zIndexOffset: 1000 })
                .bindPopup(`<strong>🏁 Punto de partida / Tu ubicación</strong><br>${escapeHtml(point.label)}`)
                .addTo(routeLayer);
        }

        function clearStartPoint() {
            if (watchId !== null) {
                navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchId);
                watchId = null;
            }
            startPoint = null;
            startPointStatus.classList.add("hidden");
            routeLayer.clearLayers();
            renderAgencyList();
            updateSelectionUI();
        }

        async function loadAgencies() {
            try {
                const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, "agencias"));
                allAgencies = [];

                snap.forEach(docSnap => {
                    const data = docSnap.data();
                    const lat = data.lat ?? data.latitud ?? null;
                    const lng = data.lng ?? data.longitud ?? null;

                    if (lat === null || lng === null) return;
                    const la = Number(lat), lo = Number(lng);
                    if (isNaN(la) || isNaN(lo) || la === 0 || lo === 0) return;
                    if (data.enabled === false) return;

                    const realId = data.idReal ?? data.idreal ?? data.codigo ?? null;

                    allAgencies.push({
                        docId: docSnap.id,
                        idReal: realId !== null ? String(realId) : null,
                        zona: data.zona ? String(data.zona).toUpperCase().trim() : "SIN ZONA",
                        direccion: String(data.direccion || "").trim(),
                        lat: la,
                        lng: lo
                    });
                });

                allAgencies.sort((a, b) =>
                    (a.idReal || "").localeCompare(b.idReal || "", undefined, { numeric: true })
                );

                const zones = new Set(allAgencies.map(a => a.zona).filter(Boolean));
                zoneFilterList.innerHTML = `<option value="ALL">Todas las zonas (${zones.size})</option>`;
                Array.from(zones).sort().forEach(z => {
                    const opt = document.createElement("option");
                    opt.value = z;
                    opt.textContent = z;
                    zoneFilterList.appendChild(opt);
                });

                renderAgencyList();
                updateTopBarStatus();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error cargando agencias:", err);
            }
        }

        function renderAgencyList() {
            const search = agencySearch.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            const zone = zoneFilterList.value;

            const filtered = allAgencies.filter(a => {
                if (zone !== "ALL" && a.zona !== zone) return false;
                if (search) {
                    const inId = (a.idReal || "").toLowerCase().includes(search);
                    const inDir = a.direccion.toLowerCase().includes(search);
                    const inZone = a.zona.toLowerCase().includes(search);
                    if (!inId && !inDir && !inZone) return false;
                }
                return true;
            });

            agencyListForRoute.innerHTML = "";

            if (filtered.length === 0) {
                agencyListForRoute.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-center py-12 text-slate-500 text-sm">
                        <i class="fa fa-inbox text-3xl mb-2 block text-slate-600"></i>
                        Sin agencias que coincidan.
                    </div>`;
                return;
            }

            filtered.forEach(a => agencyListForRoute.appendChild(buildAgencyRow(a)));
        }

        function buildAgencyRow(a) {
            const isSelected = selectedIds.has(a.docId);
            const isStart = startPoint && Math.abs(startPoint.lat - a.lat) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(startPoint.lng - a.lng) < 1e-6;

            const row = document.createElement("div");
            row.className = `agency-row${isSelected ? " selected" : ""}`;

            const cb = document.createElement("div");
            cb.className = `w-6 h-6 rounded-lg border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-all ${
                isSelected ? "bg-brand-600 border-brand-500" : "bg-slate-950 border-slate-700"
            }`;
            if (isSelected) cb.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-check text-white text-xs"></i>`;
            row.appendChild(cb);

            const content = document.createElement("div");
            content.className = "min-w-0 flex-1";

            const title = document.createElement("p");
            title.className = "text-sm font-bold text-slate-200 truncate";
            title.textContent = a.idReal || "Sin ID";
            content.appendChild(title);

            const dir = document.createElement("p");
            dir.className = "text-xs text-slate-500 truncate mt-0.5";
            dir.textContent = a.direccion || "Sin dirección";
            content.appendChild(dir);

            const zoneBadge = document.createElement("span");
            zoneBadge.className = "inline-block mt-1 text-[10px] font-mono text-brand-400 bg-brand-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded";
            zoneBadge.textContent = a.zona;
            content.appendChild(zoneBadge);

            row.appendChild(content);

            const btnStart = document.createElement("button");
            btnStart.className = `w-11 h-11 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-all text-base ${
                isStart
                    ? "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400"
                    : "text-slate-500 hover:text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-500/10 active:bg-emerald-500/20"
            }`;
            btnStart.title = isStart ? "Este es el punto de partida" : "Marcar como punto de partida";
            btnStart.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-flag-checkered"></i>`;
            btnStart.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (isStart) {
                    clearStartPoint();
                } else {
                    setStartPoint({ lat: a.lat, lng: a.lng, label: a.idReal || a.docId }, true);
                    switchSheetTab("start");
                }
            });
            row.appendChild(btnStart);

            row.addEventListener("click", () => toggleSelection(a.docId));

            return row;
        }

        function toggleSelection(id) {
            if (selectedIds.has(id)) selectedIds.delete(id);
            else selectedIds.add(id);
            updateSelectionUI();
            renderAgencyList();
        }

        function selectAllFiltered() {
            const search = agencySearch.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            const zone = zoneFilterList.value;

            allAgencies.forEach(a => {
                if (zone !== "ALL" && a.zona !== zone) return;
                if (search) {
                    const inId = (a.idReal || "").toLowerCase().includes(search);
                    const inDir = a.direccion.toLowerCase().includes(search);
                    const inZone = a.zona.toLowerCase().includes(search);
                    if (!inId && !inDir && !inZone) return;
                }
                selectedIds.add(a.docId);
            });

            updateSelectionUI();
            renderAgencyList();
        }

        function updateSelectionUI() {
            const count = selectedIds.size;
            selectionCountBig.textContent = count;

            if (count > 0) {
                tabAgencyCount.textContent = count;
                tabAgencyCount.classList.remove("hidden");
            } else {
                tabAgencyCount.classList.add("hidden");
            }

            const canCalculate = count >= 2 && startPoint;
            fabCalculate.disabled = !canCalculate;
            if (btnCalculateDesktop) {
                btnCalculateDesktop.disabled = !canCalculate;
            }

            updateTopBarStatus();
        }

        function updateTopBarStatus() {
            const count = selectedIds.size;
            if (!startPoint) {
                topBarStatus.textContent = "Falta punto de partida";
                return;
            }
            if (count < 2) {
                topBarStatus.textContent = `Selecciona 2+ agencias (${count})`;
                return;
            }
            topBarStatus.textContent = `${count} paradas listas`;
        }

        async function calculateRoute() {
            if (!startPoint) {
                Swal.fire("Falta el punto de partida", "Define desde dónde comenzar la ruta.", "warning");
                return;
            }

            const stopAgencies = allAgencies.filter(a => selectedIds.has(a.docId));
            if (stopAgencies.length < 2) {
                Swal.fire("Muy pocas paradas", "Selecciona al menos 2 agencias.", "warning");
                return;
            }

            const profile = profileInput.value;
            const roundTrip = roundTripCheck.checked;

            calcOverlay.style.display = "flex";
            updateCalcStatus("Optimizando ruta...", "Consultando distancias reales");

            try {
                const allPoints = [
                    { lat: startPoint.lat, lng: startPoint.lng, type: "start", label: startPoint.label },
                    ...stopAgencies.map(a => ({ ...a, type: "stop" }))
                ];

                updateCalcStatus("Consultando distancias reales...", "OSRM Table API");
                const matrix = await getDistanceMatrix(allPoints, profile);
                if (!matrix) throw new Error("No se pudo obtener la matriz de distancias");

                updateCalcStatus("Optimizando orden...", "Nearest Neighbor + 2-opt");
                const order = solveTSP(matrix.distances, 0);
                const orderedPoints = order.map(idx => allPoints[idx]);

                updateCalcStatus("Trazando carretera...", "OSRM Route API");
                const routeGeometry = await getRouteGeometry(orderedPoints, profile, roundTrip);

                const segments = [];
                const pointsSeq = roundTrip ? [...orderedPoints, orderedPoints[0]] : orderedPoints;

                for (let i = 0; i < pointsSeq.length - 1; i++) {
                    const from = pointsSeq[i];
                    const to = pointsSeq[i + 1];
                    const d = haversine(from.lat, from.lng, to.lat, to.lng) * 1000;
                    segments.push({ from, to, distance: d });
                }

                const totalDist = routeGeometry.distance;
                const totalDur = routeGeometry.duration;

                const sumSegments = segments.reduce((s, x) => s + x.distance, 0) || 1;
                segments.forEach(s => {
                    s.distance = (s.distance / sumSegments) * totalDist;
                    s.duration = (s.distance / totalDist) * totalDur;
                });

                lastRoute = {
                    order,
                    points: orderedPoints,
                    segments,
                    geometry: routeGeometry.geometry,
                    totalDistance: totalDist,
                    totalDuration: totalDur,
                    profile,
                    roundTrip,
                    startPoint
                };

                renderRouteOnMap(lastRoute);
                updateRoutePanel(lastRoute);
                switchSheetTab("route");

                if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([30, 50, 30]);

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error calculando ruta:", err);
                Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudo calcular la ruta: " + err.message, "error");
            } finally {
                calcOverlay.style.display = "none";
            }
        }

        function updateCalcStatus(main, sub) {
            calcStatusText.textContent = main;
            calcStatusSub.textContent = sub || "";
        }

        async function getDistanceMatrix(points, profile = "driving") {
            const coordStr = points.map(p => `${p.lng},${p.lat}`).join(";");
            const url = `${OSRM_BASE}/table/v1/${profile}/${coordStr}?annotations=distance,duration`;

            try {
                const res = await fetch(url);
                if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (data.code !== "Ok") throw new Error(data.message || data.code);
                return { distances: data.distances, durations: data.durations };
            } catch (err) {
                const n = points.length;
                const dist = Array.from({ length: n }, () => Array(n).fill(0));
                const dur = Array.from({ length: n }, () => Array(n).fill(0));
                const SPEED_KMH = profile === "walking" ? 5 : profile === "cycling" ? 18 : 45;

                for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                        if (i === j) continue;
                        const d = haversine(points[i].lat, points[i].lng, points[j].lat, points[j].lng);
                        dist[i][j] = d * 1000;
                        dur[i][j] = (d / SPEED_KMH) * 3600;
                    }
                }
                return { distances: dist, durations: dur };
            }
        }

        async function getRouteGeometry(points, profile = "driving", roundTrip = false) {
            const seq = roundTrip ? [...points, points[0]] : points;
            const coordStr = seq.map(p => `${p.lng},${p.lat}`).join(";");
            const url = `${OSRM_BASE}/route/v1/${profile}/${coordStr}?overview=full&geometries=geojson`;

            const res = await fetch(url);
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`OSRM HTTP ${res.status}`);
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.code !== "Ok") throw new Error(data.message || data.code);

            const route = data.routes[0];
            return {
                geometry: route.geometry,
                distance: route.distance,
                duration: route.duration
            };
        }

        function solveTSP(matrix, startIdx = 0) {
            const n = matrix.length;
            if (n === 0) return [];
            if (n === 1) return [0];

            const visited = new Set([startIdx]);
            const order = [startIdx];
            let current = startIdx;

            while (visited.size < n) {
                let bestIdx = -1;
                let bestDist = Infinity;
                for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    if (visited.has(i)) continue;
                    const d = matrix[current][i];
                    if (d < bestDist) {
                        bestDist = d;
                        bestIdx = i;
                    }
                }
                visited.add(bestIdx);
                order.push(bestIdx);
                current = bestIdx;
            }

            return twoOpt(order, matrix);
        }

        function twoOpt(order, matrix) {
            const n = order.length;
            let improved = true;
            let best = [...order];
            let iterations = 0;

            while (improved && iterations < 200) {
                improved = false;
                iterations++;

                for (let i = 1; i < n - 1; i++) {
                    for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
                        const a = best[i - 1], b = best[i], c = best[j], d = j + 1 < n ? best[j + 1] : -1;
                        const currentDist = matrix[a][b] + (d >= 0 ? matrix[c][d] : 0);
                        const newDist = matrix[a][c] + (d >= 0 ? matrix[b][d] : 0);

                        if (newDist < currentDist - 0.1) {
                            best = [...best.slice(0, i), ...best.slice(i, j + 1).reverse(), ...best.slice(j + 1)];
                            improved = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return best;
        }

        function renderRouteOnMap(route) {
            routeLayer.clearLayers();

            if (route.geometry && route.geometry.coordinates) {
                const coords = route.geometry.coordinates.map(([lng, lat]) => [lat, lng]);
                L.polyline(coords, {
                    color: "#6366f1",
                    weight: 5,
                    opacity: 0.85,
                    lineCap: "round",
                    lineJoin: "round"
                }).addTo(routeLayer);
            }

            route.points.forEach((point, idx) => {
                const isStart = idx === 0;
                const html = isStart
                    ? `<div class="stop-marker start"><i class="fa fa-flag-checkered"></i></div>`
                    : `<div class="stop-marker">${idx}</div>`;

                const icon = L.divIcon({
                    className: "",
                    html,
                    iconSize: isStart ? [38, 38] : [32, 32],
                    iconAnchor: isStart ? [19, 19] : [16, 16]
                });

                L.marker([point.lat, point.lng], { icon, zIndexOffset: isStart ? 1000 : 0 })
                    .bindPopup(`<strong>${isStart ? '🏁 Origen' : idx + '. ' + (point.idReal || '')}</strong><br>${escapeHtml(point.direccion || '')}`)
                    .addTo(routeLayer);
            });
        }

        function updateRoutePanel(route) {
            routeEmpty.classList.add("hidden");
            routeContent.classList.remove("hidden");

            summaryDistance.textContent = formatDistance(route.totalDistance);
            summaryDuration.textContent = formatDuration(route.totalDuration);
            summaryStops.textContent = route.points.length - 1;

            visitedStops.clear();
            renderStopsList();
            updateProgressUI();
        }

        function renderStopsList() {
            if (!lastRoute) return;
            routeStopsList.innerHTML = "";

            lastRoute.points.forEach((point, idx) => {
                const isStart = idx === 0;
                const isVisited = visitedStops.has(idx);
                const isCurrent = !isStart && !isVisited && getNextPendingIndex() === idx;
                const stateClass = isVisited ? "visited" : (isCurrent ? "current" : "pending");

                const row = document.createElement("div");
                row.className = `stop-row ${stateClass}`;

                const checkBtn = document.createElement("button");
                checkBtn.className = "check-btn shrink-0";
                checkBtn.innerHTML = isVisited ? `<i class="fa fa-check"></i>` : (isStart ? `<i class="fa fa-flag-checkered"></i>` : `<i class="fa fa-circle"></i>`);

                if (!isStart) {
                    checkBtn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        toggleStopVisited(idx);
                    });
                }
                row.appendChild(checkBtn);

                const content = document.createElement("div");
                content.className = "min-w-0 flex-1";
                content.innerHTML = `
                    <p class="stop-name text-xs font-bold text-slate-100 truncate">${isStart ? lastRoute.startPoint.label : (point.idReal || "Agencia")}</p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">${escapeHtml(point.direccion || 'Sin dirección')}</p>
                `;
                row.appendChild(content);

                const btnCenter = document.createElement("button");
                btnCenter.className = "w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-slate-500 hover:text-brand-400 hover:bg-brand-500/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0";
                btnCenter.innerHTML = `<i class="fa fa-crosshairs"></i>`;
                btnCenter.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    map.flyTo([point.lat, point.lng], 16, { duration: 0.8 });
                });
                row.appendChild(btnCenter);

                routeStopsList.appendChild(row);
            });
        }

        function getNextPendingIndex() {
            if (!lastRoute) return -1;
            for (let i = 1; i < lastRoute.points.length; i++) {
                if (!visitedStops.has(i)) return i;
            }
            return -1;
        }

        function toggleStopVisited(idx) {
            if (!lastRoute || idx === 0) return;
            if (visitedStops.has(idx)) visitedStops.delete(idx);
            else visitedStops.add(idx);

            renderStopsList();
            updateProgressUI();
        }

        function markNextStop() {
            const nextIdx = getNextPendingIndex();
            if (nextIdx !== -1) toggleStopVisited(nextIdx);
        }

        function resetProgress() {
            visitedStops.clear();
            renderStopsList();
            updateProgressUI();
        }

        function updateProgressUI() {
            if (!lastRoute) return;
            const total = lastRoute.points.length - 1;
            const done = visitedStops.size;
            const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((done / total) * 100) : 0;

            progressLabel.textContent = `${done} de ${total} visitadas`;
            progressPct.textContent =`${pct}%`;
            progressFill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
        }

        function showStopsModal() {
            if (!lastRoute) return;
            stopsModalBody.innerHTML = "";
            lastRoute.points.forEach((point, idx) => {
                const item = document.createElement("div");
                item.className = "flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs";
                item.innerHTML = `<b>#${idx}</b> <span class="truncate">${escapeHtml(point.idReal || point.label)}</span>`;
                stopsModalBody.appendChild(item);
            });
            stopsModal.classList.remove("hidden");
        }

        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // NAVEGACIÓN INTELIGENTE (GOOGLE MAPS O WAZE)
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        function openInNavigationApps() {
            if (!lastRoute || lastRoute.points.length === 0) return;
            const dest = lastRoute.points[lastRoute.points.length - 1];

            Swal.fire({
                title: '¿Cómo deseas navegar?',
                text: 'Selecciona tu aplicación favorita',
                icon: 'question',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: '<i class="fa fa-map mr-1"></i> Google Maps',
                cancelButtonText: '<i class="fa fa-car mr-1"></i> Waze',
                confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb',
                cancelButtonColor: '#00d7ff',
                background: '#0f172a',
                color: '#f8fafc',
                reverseButtons: true
            }).then((res) => {
                if (res.isConfirmed) {
                    const origin = `${lastRoute.points[0].lat},${lastRoute.points[0].lng}`;
                    const destination = `${dest.lat},${dest.lng}`;
                    window.open(`https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=${origin}&destination=${destination}&travelmode=${lastRoute.profile}`, '_blank');
                } else if (res.dismiss === Swal.DismissReason.cancel) {
                    window.open(`https://waze.com/ul?ll=${dest.lat},${dest.lng}&navigate=yes`, '_blank');
                }
            });
        }

        function exportRouteToExcel() {
            if (!lastRoute) return;
            const rows = lastRoute.points.map((p, idx) => ({
                "Orden": idx === 0 ? "Inicio" : idx,
                "ID": p.idReal || "",
                "Lat": p.lat,
                "Lng": p.lng,
                "Dirección": p.direccion || p.label || ""
            }));
            const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows);
            const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
            XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Ruta");
            XLSX.writeFile(wb, `Ruta_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.xlsx`);
        }

        function showSaveRouteModal() {
            if (!lastRoute) return;
            routeNameInput.value = `Ruta ${new Date().toLocaleDateString("es-DO")}`;
            saveRouteModal.classList.remove("hidden");
        }

        async function saveRoute() {
            if (!lastRoute) return;
            const nombre = routeNameInput.value.trim();
            if (!nombre) return;

            try {
                const stops = lastRoute.points.slice(1).map((p, idx) => ({
                    agencyId: p.docId,
                    order: idx + 1,
                    idReal: p.idReal || "",
                    lat: p.lat,
                    lng: p.lng,
                    direccion: p.direccion || ""
                }));

                await addDoc(collection(db, "rutas"), {
                    nombre,
                    startPoint: lastRoute.startPoint,
                    stops,
                    totalDistance: lastRoute.totalDistance,
                    totalDuration: lastRoute.totalDuration,
                    createdBy: auth.currentUser.uid,
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp()
                });

                saveRouteModal.classList.add("hidden");
                Swal.fire({ toast: true, position: "top-end", icon: "success", title: "Ruta guardada", timer: 2000, showConfirmButton: false });
            } catch (err) {
                Swal.fire("Error", err.message, "error");
            }
        }

        function haversine(lat1, lng1, lat2, lng2) {
            const R = 6371;
            const toRad = x => x * Math.PI / 180;
            const dLat = toRad(lat2 - lat1);
            const dLng = toRad(lng2 - lng1);
            const a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(toRad(lat1)) * Math.cos(toRad(lat2)) * Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2;
            return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
        }

        function formatDistance(m) { return m < 1000 ? `${Math.round(m)} m` : `${(m / 1000).toFixed(1)} km`; }
        function formatDuration(s) {
            if (s < 60) return `${Math.round(s)} s`;
            const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
            return m < 60 ? `${m} min` : `${Math.floor(m / 60)}h ${m % 60}m`;
        }
        function debounce(fn, ms) {
            let t;
            return (...args) => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), ms); };
        }
        function escapeHtml(str) {
            return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c]));
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
