    <!-- SweetAlert2 -->
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

    <!-- Lógica -->
    <script type="module">
import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";                    // 👈 AGREGAR
initSidebar("sidebar-container");
        import { db, auth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

        import {
            collection,
            doc,
            getDoc,
            addDoc,
            updateDoc,
            deleteDoc,
            query,
            where,
            orderBy,
            onSnapshot,
            serverTimestamp
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

        import { onAuthStateChanged, signOut } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // DOM
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const bootOverlay      = document.getElementById("bootOverlay");
        const mainContent      = document.getElementById("mainContent");
        const roleBadgeText    = document.getElementById("roleBadgeText");
        const btnReload        = document.getElementById("btnReload");
        const adminStats       = document.getElementById("adminStats");
        const adminFilters     = document.getElementById("adminFilters");
        const suggestionsList  = document.getElementById("suggestionsList");
        const panelTitle       = document.getElementById("panelTitle");
        const panelCount       = document.getElementById("panelCount");

        const suggestionForm   = document.getElementById("suggestionForm");
        const sgNombre         = document.getElementById("sgNombre");
        const sgEmail          = document.getElementById("sgEmail");
        const sgCategoria      = document.getElementById("sgCategoria");
        const sgMensaje        = document.getElementById("sgMensaje");
        const charCounter      = document.getElementById("charCounter");
        const formMessage      = document.getElementById("formMessage");
        const btnSubmit        = document.getElementById("btnSubmit");
        const btnSubmitIcon    = document.getElementById("btnSubmitIcon");
        const btnSubmitText    = document.getElementById("btnSubmitText");

        const statTotal    = document.getElementById("statTotal");
        const statPending  = document.getElementById("statPending");
        const statApproved = document.getElementById("statApproved");
        const statRejected = document.getElementById("statRejected");

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ESTADO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        let currentUser    = null;
        let currentProfile = null;
        let isAdmin        = false;
        let allSuggestions = [];        // Solo para admin
        let mySuggestions  = [];        // Solo para viewer
        let activeFilter   = "ALL";     // Para admin
        let unsubscribe    = null;

        const CATEGORY_ICONS = {
            "Nueva Integración (API, WhatsApp, ERP, etc.)": "fa-plug",
            "Nuevo Módulo / Sección": "fa-cube",
            "Mejora de Función Existente": "fa-wand-magic-sparkles",
            "Reporte o Métrica Personalizada": "fa-chart-line",
            "Otro Comentario": "fa-comment-dots"
        };

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // AUTENTICACIÓN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }

            currentUser = user;

            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));

                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    await Swal.fire("Cuenta no registrada", "Contacta al administrador.", "error");
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                currentProfile = userSnap.data();

                if (currentProfile.enabled === false) {
                    await Swal.fire("Usuario inhabilitado", "Tu acceso ha sido revocado.", "warning");
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                isAdmin = currentProfile.role === "admin";

                // Configurar UI según rol
                setupUIForRole();

                // Pre-llenar formulario
                sgNombre.value = currentProfile.username || "";
                sgEmail.value = user.email || "";

                // Cargar sugerencias
                subscribeToSuggestions();

                bootOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
                mainContent.classList.remove("hidden");

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al validar sesión:", err);
                await Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudieron verificar tus credenciales.", "error");
                window.location.replace("login.html");
            }
        });

        function setupUIForRole() {
            if (isAdmin) {
                roleBadgeText.textContent = "Vista Administrador";
                panelTitle.textContent = "Todas las Sugerencias";
                adminStats.classList.remove("hidden");
                adminStats.classList.add("grid");
                adminFilters.classList.remove("hidden");
                setActiveFilter("ALL");
            } else {
                roleBadgeText.textContent = "Tus Sugerencias";
                panelTitle.textContent = "Mis Sugerencias";
                adminStats.classList.add("hidden");
                adminFilters.classList.add("hidden");
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // SUSCRIPCIÓN EN TIEMPO REAL
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function subscribeToSuggestions() {
            if (unsubscribe) unsubscribe();

            let q;
            if (isAdmin) {
                q = query(
                    collection(db, "sugerencias"),
                    orderBy("createdAt", "desc")
                );
            } else {
                // Viewer: solo las suyas. Ordenamos en memoria para evitar índice compuesto.
                q = query(
                    collection(db, "sugerencias"),
                    where("createdBy", "==", currentUser.uid)
                );
            }

            unsubscribe = onSnapshot(
                q,
                (snapshot) => {
                    const list = [];
                    snapshot.forEach(docSnap => {
                        list.push({ id: docSnap.id, ...docSnap.data() });
                    });

                    // Ordenar en memoria si es viewer (no hay índice)
                    if (!isAdmin) {
                        list.sort((a, b) => {
                            const ta = a.createdAt?.toMillis?.() ?? 0;
                            const tb = b.createdAt?.toMillis?.() ?? 0;
                            return tb - ta;
                        });
                    }

                    if (isAdmin) {
                        allSuggestions = list;
                        updateAdminStats();
                        renderAdminList();
                    } else {
                        mySuggestions = list;
                        renderViewerList();
                    }
                },
                (error) => {
                    console.error("Error en suscripción de sugerencias:", error);
                    let msg = "No se pudieron cargar las sugerencias.";
                    if (error.code === "permission-denied") {
                        msg = "Sin permisos. Revisa las reglas de Firestore.";
                    } else if (error.code === "failed-precondition") {
                        msg = "Falta un índice compuesto en Firestore. Revisa la consola para crear el índice sugerido.";
                    }
                    suggestionsList.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="text-center py-8 text-rose-400 text-xs bg-rose-500/5 border border-rose-500/20 rounded-2xl">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation text-2xl mb-2 block"></i>
                            ${msg}
                        </div>`;
                }
            );
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // FORM: CONTADOR DE CARACTERES
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        sgMensaje.addEventListener("input", () => {
            const len = sgMensaje.value.length;
            charCounter.textContent = `${len} / 1000`;
            charCounter.className = len > 950
                ? "text-[10px] font-mono text-amber-400"
                : "text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500";
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // FORM: ENVÍO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        suggestionForm.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (btnSubmit.disabled) return;

            const nombre = sgNombre.value.trim();
            const email = sgEmail.value.trim();
            const categoria = sgCategoria.value;
            const mensaje = sgMensaje.value.trim();

            // Validaciones
            if (!nombre || !categoria || !mensaje) {
                showMessage("Completa todos los campos obligatorios (*).", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (mensaje.length < 10) {
                showMessage("El mensaje debe tener al menos 10 caracteres.", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
                showMessage("El formato del correo no es válido.", "error");
                return;
            }

            setLoading(true);
            showMessage("", "clear");

            try {
                await addDoc(collection(db, "sugerencias"), {
                    nombre,
                    email,
                    categoria,
                    mensaje,
                    status: "pendiente",
                    createdBy: currentUser.uid,
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp()
                });

                // Reset (mantener nombre y email para comodidad)
                sgCategoria.value = "";
                sgMensaje.value = "";
                charCounter.textContent = "0 / 1000";
                charCounter.className = "text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500";

                await Swal.fire({
                    toast: true,
                    position: "top-end",
                    icon: "success",
                    title: "¡Sugerencia enviada!",
                    text: "Los administradores la revisarán pronto.",
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    timer: 2800,
                    timerProgressBar: true
                });

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al enviar sugerencia:", err);
                let msg = "No se pudo enviar la sugerencia.";
                if (err.code === "permission-denied") {
                    msg = "Sin permisos. Debes estar autenticado para enviar sugerencias.";
                }
                showMessage(msg, "error");
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        });

        function setLoading(loading) {
            btnSubmit.disabled = loading;
            btnSubmitText.textContent = loading ? "Enviando..." : "Enviar Sugerencia";
            btnSubmitIcon.className = loading
                ? "fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin text-xs"
                : "fa-solid fa-paper-plane text-xs";
        }

        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            if (type === "clear") {
                formMessage.className = "hidden text-xs p-3 rounded-xl";
                formMessage.textContent = "";
                return;
            }
            formMessage.classList.remove("hidden");
            formMessage.className = type === "success"
                ? "text-xs p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400"
                : "text-xs p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-400";
            formMessage.textContent = msg;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // FILTROS ADMIN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        adminFilters.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest(".filter-btn");
            if (!btn) return;
            setActiveFilter(btn.dataset.filter);
        });

        function setActiveFilter(filter) {
            activeFilter = filter;
            document.querySelectorAll(".filter-btn").forEach(btn => {
                const isActive = btn.dataset.filter === filter;
                btn.className = isActive
                    ? "filter-btn flex-1 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold bg-indigo-600 text-white transition-all"
                    : "filter-btn flex-1 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800/60 transition-all";
            });
            renderAdminList();
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // STATS ADMIN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function updateAdminStats() {
            const total = allSuggestions.length;
            const pending = allSuggestions.filter(s => s.status === "pendiente").length;
            const approved = allSuggestions.filter(s => s.status === "aprobada").length;
            const rejected = allSuggestions.filter(s => s.status === "rechazada").length;

            statTotal.textContent = total;
            statPending.textContent = pending;
            statApproved.textContent = approved;
            statRejected.textContent = rejected;
            panelCount.textContent = total;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // RENDER: LISTA ADMIN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function renderAdminList() {
            suggestionsList.innerHTML = "";

            let filtered = allSuggestions;
            if (activeFilter !== "ALL") {
                filtered = allSuggestions.filter(s => s.status === activeFilter);
            }

            if (filtered.length === 0) {
                suggestionsList.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-center py-12 bg-slate-900/40 border border-dashed border-slate-800 rounded-2xl">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-inbox text-3xl text-slate-600 mb-2 block"></i>
                        <p class="text-slate-400 text-sm font-bold">Sin sugerencias</p>
                        <p class="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
                            ${activeFilter === "ALL" ? "Aún no hay sugerencias registradas." : "No hay sugerencias con este estado."}
                        </p>
                    </div>`;
                return;
            }

            filtered.forEach(sg => {
                suggestionsList.appendChild(buildAdminCard(sg));
            });
        }

        function buildAdminCard(sg) {
            const card = document.createElement("div");
            card.className = "fade-in bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3 hover:border-slate-700 transition-colors";

            // ── Header: nombre + status ──
            const header = document.createElement("div");
            header.className = "flex items-start justify-between gap-2";

            const who = document.createElement("div");
            who.className = "min-w-0 flex-1";

            const nombreEl = document.createElement("p");
            nombreEl.className = "text-sm font-bold text-white truncate";
            nombreEl.textContent = sg.nombre || "Anónimo";
            who.appendChild(nombreEl);

            if (sg.email) {
                const emailEl = document.createElement("p");
                emailEl.className = "text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate";
                emailEl.textContent = sg.email;
                who.appendChild(emailEl);
            }

            header.appendChild(who);
            header.appendChild(buildStatusBadge(sg.status));

            card.appendChild(header);

            // ── Meta: categoría + fecha ──
            const meta = document.createElement("div");
            meta.className = "flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap";

            const catBadge = document.createElement("span");
            catBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700/50";
            const icon = CATEGORY_ICONS[sg.categoria] || "fa-tag";
            catBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid ${icon} text-[9px]"></i>`;
            catBadge.appendChild(document.createTextNode(sg.categoria || "Sin categoría"));
            meta.appendChild(catBadge);

            const dateEl = document.createElement("span");
            dateEl.className = "text-[10px] text-slate-500";
            dateEl.textContent = timeAgo(sg.createdAt);
            meta.appendChild(dateEl);

            card.appendChild(meta);

            // ── Mensaje ──
            const msg = document.createElement("p");
            msg.className = "text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap break-words bg-slate-950/40 border border-slate-800/50 rounded-xl p-3";
            msg.textContent = sg.mensaje || "(sin mensaje)";
            card.appendChild(msg);

            // ── Nota admin (si existe) ──
            if (sg.adminNote) {
                const noteWrap = document.createElement("div");
                noteWrap.className = "flex items-start gap-2 text-[11px] text-amber-300/80 bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl p-2.5";
                noteWrap.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-note-sticky mt-0.5"></i>`;
                const noteText = document.createElement("span");
                noteText.textContent = sg.adminNote;
                noteWrap.appendChild(noteText);
                card.appendChild(noteWrap);
            }

            // ── Acciones ──
            const actions = document.createElement("div");
            actions.className = "flex items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60";

            if (sg.status !== "aprobada") {
                const btnApprove = document.createElement("button");
                btnApprove.className = "flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600/90 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95";
                btnApprove.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i> Aprobar`;
                btnApprove.addEventListener("click", () => reviewSuggestion(sg, "aprobada"));
                actions.appendChild(btnApprove);
            }

            if (sg.status !== "rechazada") {
                const btnReject = document.createElement("button");
                btnReject.className = "flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-rose-600/90 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95";
                btnReject.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i> Rechazar`;
                btnReject.addEventListener("click", () => reviewSuggestion(sg, "rechazada"));
                actions.appendChild(btnReject);
            }

            const btnDelete = document.createElement("button");
            btnDelete.className = "px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95";
            btnDelete.title = "Eliminar definitivamente";
            btnDelete.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>`;
            btnDelete.addEventListener("click", () => deleteSuggestion(sg));
            actions.appendChild(btnDelete);

            card.appendChild(actions);

            return card;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // RENDER: LISTA VIEWER
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function renderViewerList() {
            suggestionsList.innerHTML = "";
            panelCount.textContent = mySuggestions.length;

            if (mySuggestions.length === 0) {
                suggestionsList.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-center py-12 bg-slate-900/40 border border-dashed border-slate-800 rounded-2xl">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-lightbulb text-3xl text-slate-600 mb-2 block"></i>
                        <p class="text-slate-400 text-sm font-bold">Aún no has enviado sugerencias</p>
                        <p class="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">Usa el formulario de la izquierda para enviar la primera.</p>
                    </div>`;
                return;
            }

            mySuggestions.forEach(sg => {
                suggestionsList.appendChild(buildViewerCard(sg));
            });
        }

        function buildViewerCard(sg) {
            const card = document.createElement("div");
            card.className = "fade-in bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3";

            // Header
            const header = document.createElement("div");
            header.className = "flex items-start justify-between gap-2";

            const catBadge = document.createElement("span");
            catBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700/50 min-w-0 truncate";
            const icon = CATEGORY_ICONS[sg.categoria] || "fa-tag";
            catBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid ${icon} text-[9px] shrink-0"></i>`;
            const catText = document.createElement("span");
            catText.className = "truncate";
            catText.textContent = sg.categoria || "Sin categoría";
            catBadge.appendChild(catText);
            header.appendChild(catBadge);

            header.appendChild(buildStatusBadge(sg.status));
            card.appendChild(header);

            // Mensaje
            const msg = document.createElement("p");
            msg.className = "text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap break-words bg-slate-950/40 border border-slate-800/50 rounded-xl p-3";
            msg.textContent = sg.mensaje || "(sin mensaje)";
            card.appendChild(msg);

            // Nota del admin (si existe) — útil para el usuario
            if (sg.adminNote) {
                const noteWrap = document.createElement("div");
                noteWrap.className = "flex items-start gap-2 text-[11px] text-amber-300/80 bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl p-2.5";
                noteWrap.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-comment-dots mt-0.5"></i>`;
                const noteText = document.createElement("span");
                noteText.textContent = sg.adminNote;
                noteWrap.appendChild(noteText);
                card.appendChild(noteWrap);
            }

            // Footer: fecha + review date
            const footer = document.createElement("div");
            footer.className = "flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[10px] text-slate-500 pt-1 border-t border-slate-800/60";

            const created = document.createElement("span");
            created.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-regular fa-clock mr-1"></i>`;
            created.appendChild(document.createTextNode(`Enviada ${timeAgo(sg.createdAt)}`));
            footer.appendChild(created);

            if (sg.reviewedAt && sg.status !== "pendiente") {
                const reviewed = document.createElement("span");
                reviewed.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-check-double mr-1"></i>`;
                reviewed.appendChild(document.createTextNode(`Revisada ${timeAgo(sg.reviewedAt)}`));
                footer.appendChild(reviewed);
            }

            card.appendChild(footer);

            return card;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // BADGE DE STATUS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function buildStatusBadge(status) {
            const badge = document.createElement("span");
            badge.className = "shrink-0 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider";

            const map = {
                pendiente: {
                    cls: "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20",
                    icon: "fa-clock",
                    label: "Pendiente"
                },
                aprobada: {
                    cls: "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20",
                    icon: "fa-check",
                    label: "Aprobada"
                },
                rechazada: {
                    cls: "bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/20",
                    icon: "fa-xmark",
                    label: "Rechazada"
                }
            };

            const cfg = map[status] || map.pendiente;
            badge.className += " " + cfg.cls;
            badge.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid ${cfg.icon} text-[8px]"></i>${cfg.label}`;
            return badge;
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ACCIONES ADMIN: APROBAR / RECHAZAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        async function reviewSuggestion(sg, newStatus) {
            const isApprove = newStatus === "aprobada";
            const label = isApprove ? "aprobar" : "rechazar";
            const color = isApprove ? "#10b981" : "#e11d48";

            const res = await Swal.fire({
                title: isApprove ? "¿Aprobar sugerencia?" : "¿Rechazar sugerencia?",
                html: `
                    <div class="text-left text-sm space-y-2">
                        <p class="text-slate-600">Sugerencia de <b>${escapeHtml(sg.nombre || "Anónimo")}</b></p>
                        <div class="bg-slate-100 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs text-slate-700 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">
                            ${escapeHtml(sg.mensaje || "")}
                        </div>
                        <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mt-2">
                            Nota interna (opcional, el autor la verá):
                        </label>
                        <textarea id="swal-note" rows="2" maxlength="300"
                            class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded-lg p-2 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                            placeholder="Ej: Excelente idea, la implementaremos pronto..."></textarea>
                    </div>
                `,
                icon: "question",
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: isApprove ? "Sí, aprobar" : "Sí, rechazar",
                cancelButtonText: "Cancelar",
                confirmButtonColor: color,
                cancelButtonColor: "#475569",
                preConfirm: () => {
                    return document.getElementById("swal-note").value.trim();
                }
            });

            if (!res.isConfirmed) return;

            const adminNote = res.value || "";

            try {
                await updateDoc(doc(db, "sugerencias", sg.id), {
                    status: newStatus,
                    adminNote,
                    reviewedBy: currentUser.uid,
                    reviewedAt: serverTimestamp()
                });

                Swal.fire({
                    toast: true,
                    position: "top-end",
                    icon: "success",
                    title: isApprove ? "Sugerencia aprobada" : "Sugerencia rechazada",
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    timer: 2000,
                    timerProgressBar: true
                });

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al revisar sugerencia:", err);
                Swal.fire("Error", `No se pudo ${label} la sugerencia.`, "error");
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ACCIÓN ADMIN: ELIMINAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        async function deleteSuggestion(sg) {
            const res = await Swal.fire({
                title: "¿Eliminar sugerencia?",
                html: `
                    <div class="text-left text-sm">
                        <p>Esta acción es <b class="text-rose-500">permanente</b> y no se puede deshacer.</p>
                        <div class="bg-slate-100 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs text-slate-700 mt-2 max-h-24 overflow-y-auto">
                            ${escapeHtml(sg.mensaje || "")}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `,
                icon: "warning",
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: "Sí, eliminar",
                cancelButtonText: "Cancelar",
                confirmButtonColor: "#e11d48",
                cancelButtonColor: "#475569"
            });

            if (!res.isConfirmed) return;

            try {
                await deleteDoc(doc(db, "sugerencias", sg.id));
                Swal.fire({
                    toast: true,
                    position: "top-end",
                    icon: "success",
                    title: "Sugerencia eliminada",
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    timer: 1800
                });
            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al eliminar:", err);
                Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudo eliminar la sugerencia.", "error");
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // RECARGAR
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        btnReload.addEventListener("click", () => {
            btnReload.disabled = true;
            btnReload.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin text-xs"></i>`;
            subscribeToSuggestions();
            setTimeout(() => {
                btnReload.disabled = false;
                btnReload.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-right text-xs"></i>`;
            }, 800);
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // UTILIDADES
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function timeAgo(ts) {
            if (!ts) return "hace un momento";
            const date = ts.toDate ? ts.toDate() : new Date(ts);
            const diff = Math.floor((Date.now() - date.getTime()) / 1000);

            if (diff < 60) return "hace unos segundos";
            const min = Math.floor(diff / 60);
            if (min < 60) return `hace ${min} min`;
            const h = Math.floor(min / 60);
            if (h < 24) return `hace ${h} h`;
            const d = Math.floor(h / 24);
            if (d < 30) return `hace ${d} d`;
            return date.toLocaleDateString("es-DO");
        }

        function escapeHtml(str) {
            return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
                "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;"
            }[c]));
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <!-- SCRIPT PRINCIPAL -->
    <script type="module">
        import { initSidebar } from "./js/sidebar.js";                    // 👈 AGREGAR
        initSidebar("sidebar-container");
        import { db, auth, secondaryAuth, clearSecondaryAuth } from "./js/firebase/firebase.init.js";

        import {
            createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
            onAuthStateChanged,
            signOut
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-auth.js";

        import {
            collection,
            doc,
            getDoc,
            setDoc,
            updateDoc,
            deleteDoc,
            onSnapshot,
            query,
            orderBy,
            serverTimestamp
        } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.12.2/firebase-firestore.js";

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // MÓDULOS DISPONIBLES EN EL SISTEMA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const AVAILABLE_MODULES = [
            { key: "index", label: "Dashboard", icon: "fa-house" },
            { key: "mapa", label: "Mapa", icon: "fa-map-location-dot" },
            { key: "cercana", label: "Cercanas", icon: "fa-location-dot" },
            { key: "rutas", label: "Rutas", icon: "fa-route" },
            { key: "reportes", label: "Reportes", icon: "fa-chart-pie" },
            { key: "imprimir", label: "Imprimir", icon: "fa-print" },
            { key: "sugerencias", label: "Sugerencias", icon: "fa-lightbulb" },
            { key: "acerca", label: "Acerca de", icon: "fa-circle-info" }
        ];

        // Módulos por defecto para un viewer sin configuración previa
        const DEFAULT_VIEWER_MODULES = ["index", "mapa", "reportes", "rutas", "sugerencias", "cercana"];



        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // ELEMENTOS DOM
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const securityOverlay = document.getElementById("securityOverlay");
        const mainContent = document.getElementById("mainContent");
        const createUserForm = document.getElementById("create-user-form");
        const usersList = document.getElementById("users-list");
        const adminEmailDisplay = document.getElementById("admin-email");
        const formMessage = document.getElementById("form-message");
        const userCount = document.getElementById("user-count");
        const btnSubmit = document.getElementById("btn-submit");
        const btnSubmitText = document.getElementById("btn-submit-text");
        const btnSubmitIcon = document.getElementById("btn-submit-icon");
        const toggleRegPassword = document.getElementById("toggleRegPassword");
        const regPasswordInput = document.getElementById("reg-password");

        let currentAdminUid = null;
        let unsubscribeSnapshot = null;

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // TOGGLE VISIBILIDAD DE CONTRASEÑA
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        toggleRegPassword.addEventListener("click", () => {
            const isPassword = regPasswordInput.type === "password";
            regPasswordInput.type = isPassword ? "text" : "password";
            toggleRegPassword.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-eye${isPassword ? "-slash" : ""} text-xs" aria-hidden="true"></i>`;
            regPasswordInput.focus();
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // RENDER CHECKBOXES DE MÓDULOS (FORM CREAR)
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function renderModulesGrid() {
            const grid = document.getElementById("modules-grid");
            if (!grid) return;
            grid.innerHTML = "";

            AVAILABLE_MODULES.forEach(mod => {
                const label = document.createElement("label");
                label.className = "flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-300 cursor-pointer p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-900/60 transition";

                const cb = document.createElement("input");
                cb.type = "checkbox";
                cb.value = mod.key;
                cb.className = "accent-indigo-500 w-4 h-4";
                cb.checked = DEFAULT_VIEWER_MODULES.includes(mod.key);

                const icon = document.createElement("i");
                icon.className = `fa-solid ${mod.icon} text-[10px] text-indigo-400 w-3.5 text-center`;

                const txt = document.createElement("span");
                txt.textContent = mod.label;

                label.appendChild(cb);
                label.appendChild(icon);
                label.appendChild(txt);
                grid.appendChild(label);
            });
        }

        function getSelectedModules() {
            const grid = document.getElementById("modules-grid");
            if (!grid) return [];
            return Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked'))
                .map(cb => cb.value);
        }

        // Render al cargar
        renderModulesGrid();

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // 1. VERIFICACIÓN DE SESIÓN Y ROL ADMIN
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
            // 1.1 Sin sesión → redirigir a login
            if (!user) {
                window.location.replace("login.html");
                return;
            }

            // 1.2 Validar que exista y sea admin
            try {
                const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", user.uid));

                if (!userSnap.exists()) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Tu cuenta no está registrada en el sistema. Contacta al administrador.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                const userData = userSnap.data();

                if (userData.enabled === false) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Tu usuario ha sido inhabilitado.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    await signOut(auth);
                    window.location.replace("login.html");
                    return;
                }

                if (userData.role !== "admin") {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: '¡Error!',
                        text: 'Acceso denegado: Solo los administradores pueden gestionar usuarios.',
                        confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                    });
                    window.location.replace("index.html");
                    return;
                }

                // 1.3 Permiso concedido
                currentAdminUid = user.uid;
                adminEmailDisplay.innerText = user.email;
                securityOverlay.classList.add("hidden");
                mainContent.classList.remove("hidden");

                // 1.4 Cargar lista de usuarios en tiempo real
                subscribeToUsers();

            } catch (err) {
                console.error("Error al validar permisos:", err);
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: '¡Error!',
                    text: 'Ocurrió un error al verificar tus credenciales.',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar'
                });
                window.location.replace("index.html");
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // 2. SUSCRIPCIÓN EN TIEMPO REAL A USUARIOS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        function subscribeToUsers() {
            // Evita múltiples suscripciones si onAuthStateChanged se dispara varias veces
            if (unsubscribeSnapshot) unsubscribeSnapshot();

            const usersQuery = query(
                collection(db, "users"),
                orderBy("createdAt", "desc")
            );

            unsubscribeSnapshot = onSnapshot(
                usersQuery,
                (snapshot) => {
                    usersList.innerHTML = "";
                    userCount.textContent = `${snapshot.size} usuario(s)`;

                    if (snapshot.empty) {
                        usersList.innerHTML = `
                            <div class="text-center py-8 text-slate-500 text-sm">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-user-slash text-2xl mb-2 block text-slate-600"></i>
                                No hay usuarios registrados todavía.
                            </div>`;
                        return;
                    }

                    snapshot.forEach((docSnap) => {
                        usersList.appendChild(createUserCard(docSnap.id, docSnap.data()));
                    });
                },
                (error) => {
                    console.error("Error en snapshot de usuarios:", error);
                    usersList.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="text-center py-8 text-rose-400 text-sm">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation text-2xl mb-2 block"></i>
                            Error al cargar usuarios. Revisa los permisos de Firestore.
                        </div>`;
                }
            );
        }

// ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. CREAR CARD DE USUARIO
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────
function createUserCard(id, data) {
    const card = document.createElement("div");
    card.className = "flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl hover:border-slate-700/80 transition gap-4";

    const isEnabled = data.enabled !== false;
    const isCurrentUser = id === currentAdminUid;
    const displayName = data.username || "Sin Nombre";
    const email = data.email || "Sin correo";
    const role = data.role === "admin" ? "admin" : "viewer";

    // ── Módulos del usuario (con fallback) ──
    const userModules = Array.isArray(data.modulos)
        ? data.modulos
        : (role === "admin" ? AVAILABLE_MODULES.map(m => m.key) : DEFAULT_VIEWER_MODULES);

    const canPrint = userModules.includes("imprimir");

    const roleBadge = role === "admin"
        ? `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400 ring-1 ring-inset ring-indigo-500/20">
               <i class="fa-solid fa-shield-halved text-[9px]"></i> Admin
           </span>`
        : `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-700/40 text-slate-300 ring-1 ring-inset ring-slate-600/40">
               <i class="fa-solid fa-eye text-[9px]"></i> Viewer
           </span>`;

    const statusBadge = isEnabled
        ? `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 ring-1 ring-inset ring-emerald-500/20">
               <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400"></span> Activo
           </span>`
        : `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 ring-1 ring-inset ring-rose-500/20">
               <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-400"></span> Inactivo
           </span>`;

    const selfBadge = isCurrentUser
        ? `<span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400 bg-amber-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ring-1 ring-inset ring-amber-500/20">Tú</span>`
        : "";

    const printBadge = canPrint
        ? `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 ring-1 ring-inset ring-emerald-500/20" title="Puede imprimir">
               <i class="fa-solid fa-print text-[8px]"></i>
           </span>`
        : "";

    // Etiquetas cortas de módulos (máximo 3 visibles)
    const moduleLabels = userModules.slice(0, 3).map(key => {
        const mod = AVAILABLE_MODULES.find(m => m.key === key);
        return mod ? mod.label : key;
    });
    const moreCount = userModules.length - moduleLabels.length;

    const modulesTooltip = userModules
        .map(key => {
            const mod = AVAILABLE_MODULES.find(m => m.key === key);
            return mod ? mod.label : key;
        })
        .join(", ");

    card.innerHTML = `
        <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-100 truncate">${escapeHtml(displayName)}</h3>
                ${roleBadge}
                ${statusBadge}
                ${selfBadge}
            </div>

            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">${escapeHtml(email)}</p>

            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 flex-wrap">
                <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded" title="UID">
                    ${id.substring(0, 12)}…
                </span>

                <span class="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Módulos:</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-300 bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded" title="${escapeHtml(modulesTooltip)}">
                    ${userModules.length}
                </span>

                ${moduleLabels.map(lbl => `
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-semibold text-slate-400 bg-slate-800/60 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        ${escapeHtml(lbl)}
                    </span>
                `).join("")}

                ${moreCount > 0 ? `
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-semibold text-slate-500 bg-slate-800/40 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded" title="${escapeHtml(modulesTooltip)}">
                        +${moreCount}
                    </span>
                ` : ""}

                ${printBadge}
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            <button data-action="perms" data-id="${id}"
                title="Editar módulos permitidos"
                class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/20 text-purple-400 hover:bg-purple-500/20 text-xs transition">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-sliders"></i>
            </button>

            <button data-action="role" data-id="${id}" data-role="${role}"
                title="Cambiar rol"
                class="p-2 rounded-lg border text-xs transition bg-indigo-500/10 border-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400 hover:bg-indigo-500/20">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-shield-halved"></i>
            </button>

            <button data-action="toggle" data-id="${id}" data-status="${isEnabled}"
                title="${isEnabled ? "Inhabilitar" : "Habilitar"}"
                ${isCurrentUser ? "disabled" : ""}
                class="p-2 rounded-lg border text-xs transition disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed ${
                    isEnabled
                        ? "bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500/20"
                        : "bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-500/20"
                }">
                <i class="fa-solid ${isEnabled ? "fa-user-slash" : "fa-user-check"}"></i>
            </button>

            <button data-action="delete" data-id="${id}"
                title="Eliminar usuario del sistema"
                ${isCurrentUser ? "disabled" : ""}
                class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-500/20 text-xs transition disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
                <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
            </button>
        </div>
    `;

    // ── Event listeners ──
    card.querySelector('[data-action="perms"]').addEventListener("click", () => {
        openPermissionsModal(id, displayName, userModules);
    });

    card.querySelector('[data-action="role"]').addEventListener("click", () => {
        changeUserRole(id, role, displayName);
    });

    card.querySelector('[data-action="toggle"]').addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        if (e.currentTarget.disabled) return;
        toggleUserStatus(id, isEnabled, displayName);
    });

    card.querySelector('[data-action="delete"]').addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        if (e.currentTarget.disabled) return;
        deleteUserRecord(id, displayName, email);
    });

    return card;
}


        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // 4. CREAR NUEVO USUARIO
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        createUserForm.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (btnSubmit.disabled) return;

            const username = document.getElementById("reg-username").value.trim();
            const email = document.getElementById("reg-email").value.trim();
            const password = document.getElementById("reg-password").value;
            const role = document.getElementById("reg-role").value;

            // Validación mínima (por si el navegador no la aplicó por novalidate)
            if (!username || !email || password.length < 6) {
                showMessage("Completa todos los campos. La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres.", "error");
                return;
            }

            setLoading(true);
            showMessage("", "clear");

            try {
                // 4.1 Crear usuario en Auth usando la app SECUNDARIA
                //     para NO desloguear al admin actual.
                const userCredential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(secondaryAuth, email, password);
                const newUser = userCredential.user;

                // 4.2 Limpiar sesión secundaria
                await clearSecondaryAuth();

                // 4.3 Guardar datos extendidos en Firestore (con rol)
                await setDoc(doc(db, "users", newUser.uid), {
                    username,
                    email,
                    role,
                    modulos: role === "admin" ? AVAILABLE_MODULES.map(m => m.key) : getSelectedModules(),
                    enabled: true,
                    createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
                    createdBy: currentAdminUid
                });

                showMessage(`Usuario "${username}" creado correctamente como ${role === "admin" ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador"}.`, "success");
                createUserForm.reset();

            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Error al crear usuario:", error);
                showMessage(getFriendlyError(error), "error");
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        });

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // 5. ACCIONES SOBRE USUARIOS
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────


        async function toggleUserStatus(uid, currentStatus, username) {
            const action = currentStatus ? "inhabilitar" : "habilitar";

            const result = await Swal.fire({
                title: `¿${currentStatus ? "Inhabilitar" : "Habilitar"} usuario?`,
                html: `¿Seguro que deseas ${action} al usuario <strong>"${username}"</strong>?`,
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: currentStatus ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a',
                cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
                confirmButtonText: currentStatus
                    ? 'Sí, inhabilitar'
                    : 'Sí, habilitar',
                cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar',
                reverseButtons: true,
                focusCancel: true
            });

            if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

            try {
                Swal.fire({
                    title: 'Actualizando estado...',
                    text: 'Por favor, espera.',
                    allowOutsideClick: false,
                    allowEscapeKey: false,
                    didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading()
                });

                await updateDoc(doc(db, "users", uid), {
                    enabled: !currentStatus,
                    updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                });

                await Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: currentStatus ? '¡Usuario inhabilitado!' : '¡Usuario habilitado!',
                    text: currentStatus
                        ? `El usuario "${username}" ha sido inhabilitado correctamente.`
                        : `El usuario "${username}" ha sido habilitado correctamente.`,
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
                    confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb',
                    timer: 2500,
                    timerProgressBar: true
                });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error al cambiar el estado del usuario:', error);

                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: '¡Error!',
                    text: 'No se pudo cambiar el estado del usuario.',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
                    confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
                });
            }
        }



        async function changeUserRole(uid, currentRole, username) {
            const newRole = currentRole === "admin" ? "viewer" : "admin";
            const label = newRole === "admin" ? "Administrador" : "Visualizador";

            const result = await Swal.fire({
                title: '¿Cambiar rol de usuario?',
                html: `¿Deseas cambiar el rol de <strong>"${username}"</strong> a <strong>${label}</strong>?`,
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: newRole === "admin" ? '#16a34a' : '#2563eb',
                cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
                confirmButtonText: `Sí, cambiar a ${label}`,
                cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar',
                reverseButtons: true,
                focusCancel: true
            });

            if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

            try {
                Swal.fire({
                    title: 'Actualizando rol...',
                    text: 'Por favor, espera.',
                    allowOutsideClick: false,
                    allowEscapeKey: false,
                    didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading()
                });

                await updateDoc(doc(db, "users", uid), {
                    role: newRole,
                    updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                });

                await Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: '¡Rol actualizado!',
                    html: `El usuario <strong>"${username}"</strong> ahora tiene el rol de <strong>${label}</strong>.`,
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
                    confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb',
                    timer: 2500,
                    timerProgressBar: true
                });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error al cambiar el rol del usuario:', error);

                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: '¡Error!',
                    text: 'No se pudo cambiar el rol del usuario. Inténtalo nuevamente.',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
                    confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
                });
            }
        }



        async function deleteUserRecord(uid, username, email) {
            const result = await Swal.fire({
                title: '¿Eliminar usuario?',
                html: `
            <p>¿Seguro que deseas eliminar a <strong>"${username}"</strong> del sistema?</p><br>
            <p style="text-align: left; color: #dc2626; font-weight: bold;">
                
            IMPORTANTE:
            </p><br>
            <ul style="text-align: left; padding-left: 20px;">
                <li>Se eliminará su perfil y sus permisos de Firestore.</li>
                <li>Su cuenta de Firebase Authentication seguirá existiendo, pero no podrá acceder si tu sistema bloquea los usuarios sin perfil.</li>
                <li>Para eliminar completamente su cuenta de Authentication, debes hacerlo desde Firebase Console o mediante un backend autorizado.</li>
            </ul>
            <p style="color: #dc2626;">Esta acción no se puede deshacer fácilmente.</p>
        `,
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: '#dc2626',
                cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
                confirmButtonText: '<i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Sí, eliminar',
                cancelButtonText: 'Cancelar',
                reverseButtons: true,
                focusCancel: true
            });

            if (!result.isConfirmed) return;

            try {
                Swal.fire({
                    title: 'Eliminando usuario...',
                    text: 'Por favor, espera.',
                    allowOutsideClick: false,
                    allowEscapeKey: false,
                    didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading()
                });

                await deleteDoc(doc(db, "users", uid));

                await Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: '¡Registro eliminado!',
                    text: `El perfil de "${username}" se eliminó correctamente.`,
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
                    confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb',
                    timer: 2500,
                    timerProgressBar: true
                });

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error al eliminar el registro del usuario:', error);

                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: '¡Error!',
                    text: 'No se pudo eliminar el registro del usuario. Inténtalo nuevamente.',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Aceptar',
                    confirmButtonColor: '#2563eb'
                });
            }
        }

        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODAL DE PERMISOS DE MÓDULOS
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────
const permsModal        = document.getElementById("permsModal");
const permsUidInput     = document.getElementById("permsUid");
const permsUserSpan     = document.getElementById("permsUser");
const permsGrid         = document.getElementById("permsGrid");
const btnSavePerms      = document.getElementById("btnSavePerms");
const btnSavePermsIcon  = document.getElementById("btnSavePermsIcon");
const btnSavePermsText  = document.getElementById("btnSavePermsText");

/**
 * Abre el modal de permisos para un usuario.
 * @param {string} uid           - UID de Firebase Auth del usuario
 * @param {string} username      - Nombre a mostrar en el título
 * @param {string[]} activeModules - Módulos actualmente habilitados
 */
function openPermissionsModal(uid, username, activeModules) {
    permsUidInput.value = uid;
    permsUserSpan.textContent = username;
    permsGrid.innerHTML = "";

    AVAILABLE_MODULES.forEach(mod => {
        const label = document.createElement("label");
        label.className = "flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-300 cursor-pointer p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-900/60 transition";

        const cb = document.createElement("input");
        cb.type = "checkbox";
        cb.value = mod.key;
        cb.className = "accent-purple-500 w-4 h-4";
        cb.checked = activeModules.includes(mod.key);

        const icon = document.createElement("i");
        icon.className = `fa-solid ${mod.icon} text-[10px] text-purple-400 w-3.5 text-center`;

        const txt = document.createElement("span");
        txt.textContent = mod.label;

        label.appendChild(cb);
        label.appendChild(icon);
        label.appendChild(txt);
        permsGrid.appendChild(label);
    });

    permsModal.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function closePermissionsModal() {
    permsModal.classList.add("hidden");
    permsUidInput.value = "";
    permsGrid.innerHTML = "";
}

// ── Listeners del modal ──
document.getElementById("btnClosePerms").addEventListener("click", closePermissionsModal);
document.getElementById("btnCancelPerms").addEventListener("click", closePermissionsModal);

permsModal.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target === permsModal) closePermissionsModal();
});

document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (e.key === "Escape" && !permsModal.classList.contains("hidden")) {
        closePermissionsModal();
    }
});

// ── Guardar permisos ──
btnSavePerms.addEventListener("click", async () => {
    if (btnSavePerms.disabled) return;

    const uid = permsUidInput.value;
    if (!uid) return;

    const selected = Array.from(permsGrid.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked'))
        .map(cb => cb.value);

    btnSavePerms.disabled = true;
    btnSavePermsIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin";
    btnSavePermsText.textContent = "Guardando...";

    try {
        await updateDoc(doc(db, "users", uid), {
            modulos: selected,
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
        });

        closePermissionsModal();

        await Swal.fire({
            toast: true,
            position: "top-end",
            icon: "success",
            title: "Permisos actualizados",
            text: `${selected.length} módulo${selected.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} asignado${selected.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`,
            showConfirmButton: false,
            timer: 2200,
            timerProgressBar: true
        });

    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error al guardar permisos:", err);
        Swal.fire("Error", "No se pudieron guardar los permisos: " + err.message, "error");
    } finally {
        btnSavePerms.disabled = false;
        btnSavePermsIcon.className = "fa-solid fa-floppy-disk";
        btnSavePermsText.textContent = "Guardar Permisos";
    }
});


        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // 6. UTILIDADES
        // ────────────────────────────────────────────────

        function getFriendlyError(error) {
            switch (error.code) {
                case "auth/email-already-in-use":
                    return "El correo ya está registrado en el sistema.";
                case "auth/invalid-email":
                    return "El formato del correo no es válido.";
                case "auth/weak-password":
                    return "La contraseña es demasiado débil. Usa al menos 6 caracteres.";
                case "auth/network-request-failed":
                    return "Sin conexión. Verifica tu red e intenta de nuevo.";
                case "permission-denied":
                    return "No tienes permisos para crear usuarios. Verifica las reglas de Firestore.";
                default:
                    return "Ocurrió un error al registrar el usuario. Intenta de nuevo.";
            }
        }

        function setLoading(loading) {
            btnSubmit.disabled = loading;
            btnSubmitText.textContent = loading ? "Creando..." : "Crear Usuario";
            btnSubmitIcon.className = loading
                ? "fa-solid fa-circle-notch fa-spin"
                : "fa-solid fa-plus";
        }

        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            if (type === "clear") {
                formMessage.className = "hidden text-xs p-3 rounded-xl";
                formMessage.textContent = "";
                return;
            }
            formMessage.classList.remove("hidden");
            formMessage.className = type === "success"
                ? "text-xs p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400"
                : "text-xs p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 text-rose-400";
            formMessage.textContent = msg;
        }

        /**
         * Escapa HTML para prevenir XSS en username / email.
         */
        function escapeHtml(str) {
            return String(str ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({
                "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;"
            }[c]));
        }
    </script>
</body>

</html>
